# 第6級第四批｜6A-15《帝國預言官只能看見失敗的未來》

**The Prophet of Failed Futures**

**類型：** 宮廷智鬥／風險預言  
**級距：** Level 6（5000–6000 字彙）  
**版本：** 拾頁 StorySteps｜第四批｜25 章完整教學初稿  
**正式配詞：** L6_A本_25章配詞.md，501 個原始 ID

## 故事簡介

瑟拉只能看見失敗的未來：倒塌的橋、燃燒的條約和死亡的皇帝。當預言被拿來替侵略開脫，她開始測試那些從不准人民避開的災厄。

**English premise:** Sera can see only failed futures: the bridge that falls, the treaty that burns, the emperor who dies. When her predictions become excuses for conquest, she begins testing the futures no one has ever been allowed to avoid.

## 世界規則

瑟拉只能看見特定條件下最糟但可能發生的分支。條件改變，預言也會改變，而每次窺視未來都會奪走一段無法靠魔法取回的普通記憶。

**English:** Sera could witness the worst plausible branch only under the conditions shown to her. A vision could change when those conditions changed, and every look ahead took one ordinary memory she could never recover by magic.

## 閱讀方法與編輯說明

每一個 EN 英文段落後緊接繁中對照段落；詞彙證據句須出現在同章英文正文中。部分高階配詞取自本專案既有教學語料，依新題材整理，**尚未完成逐句文學編輯及語意終校**。本書為完整章節的教學初稿，並非出版定稿。第10章設有核心反轉，第25章主線收束。中文詞義依情境，不代表配詞原檔官方翻譯。

---

## CHAPTER 01｜The Day the Victory Burned｜勝利燃燒的那一天

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：621**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Sera saw the bridge collapse while the empire was still cheering. In her vision, soldiers rode home beneath white banners, a little girl dropped a flower, and the central arch folded into the river. By the time the dead reached the surface, the victory songs had not even stopped. Then the prophecy lens went dark. Sera was back inside the imperial observatory, her eyelid twitching with the effort of remembering something she had just lost. The emperor demanded a favorable forecast. She could see only failures. “Close the bridge,” she answered. “Now.”

**繁中** 帝國仍在歡呼時，瑟拉已看見橋梁倒塌。預言裡，士兵舉著白旗騎馬歸來，一名女孩掉落手中的花，中央橋拱隨即折入河水。死者浮上水面時，凱旋歌甚至還沒停。預言透鏡接著暗去。瑟拉回到帝國觀星臺，眼皮因努力回想剛剛失去的記憶而抽動。皇帝要求吉祥預報，她卻只能看見失敗。「封橋，」她回答，「現在。」

**EN** The order should have been applicable to every traveler, including officers with royal warrants, but the marshal had already arranged a parade. An archive clerk tried to abbreviate the list of expected guests. Sera refused. She needed to know precisely who would be standing on the bridge and how many people could still be moved. Historian Tarin brought a comparative drawing of the structure made before and after the last repair. The bronze anchor holding the central arch had been replaced with a cheaper copy. No demon had attacked it. No prophecy had broken it. Someone had signed for poor workmanship and called the result safe.

**繁中** 封橋命令本應適用每位旅人，包括持王室令狀的軍官，但元帥已安排好凱旋遊行。檔案書記想縮短預計到場者名單，瑟拉不肯。她需要知道橋上會站哪些人，以及還能撤走多少。史官塔林帶來維修前後的比較圖。固定中央橋拱的銅錨已被廉價替件取代。沒有惡魔攻擊橋梁，也不是預言把它弄壞。有人核准了粗劣施工，卻聲稱結果安全。

**EN** By the river, volunteers were broiling fish for the returning families. An injured ferryman walked with a limp as he helped an elderly widow cross the market. She pressed her grandson against her bosom, afraid of another alarm. An orderly helped people waiting in line. At a donation tent, a young soldier awkwardly asked for clean clothes for his sister, who had lost even her bra when the barracks burned. The city wanted the victory to flourish; the people simply wanted to reach home alive. Sera watched the bridge from a distance. A prediction could not move a stone or support a child. Its usefulness depended on what the living chose to do.

**繁中** 河邊志工正炙烤魚，供返鄉家庭食用。一名受傷渡船夫跛著腳，協助年長寡婦穿過市集。寡婦把孫子抱在胸前，害怕警報再次響起。捐衣帳篷旁，年輕士兵尷尬地替妹妹索取乾淨衣物；營房失火後她甚至失去胸罩。城市想讓勝利繁榮，人民只想平安回家。瑟拉遠望橋梁。預言不能搬動石頭，也不能支撐孩子，是否有用取決於活人接下來的選擇。

**EN** Tarin found a bank teller who had paid the bridge contractor from the military account. The teller said that a gangster had offered him a bribe to move the payment date back by one month. He refused and had been threatened ever since. Tarin asked whether he would narrate the incident in a safe hearing. The man stared toward the junction where the barracks road met the market; a pair of armed guards waited there. Sera did not claim that fear proved the witness correct. She asked him to describe only what he had seen, then sent the report to an independent engineer.

**繁中** 塔林找到一名出納，他曾從軍方帳戶支付橋梁承包商款項。出納說有黑幫分子向他行賄，要求把付款日期提前一個月。他拒絕後便不斷遭威脅。塔林問他是否願意在安全聽證中敘述經過。男人望向營房道路和市集交會處，一對持武器守衛正站在那裡。瑟拉沒有說恐懼能證明證人正確，只請他描述親眼所見，再把報告交給獨立工程師。

**EN** The marshal arrived with a fresh order on virgin parchment. It accused Sera of sabotaging the imperial celebration and demanded her arrest before sundown. She read the charge aloud so that no one could later invent a different one. “My visions have a finite cost,” she told him. “Every time I look, I lose a memory. If I can prevent a disaster without losing another, I will.” Marshal Dorian called her disloyal. Tarin asked why his engineers had ignored the broken anchor. Dorian answered with a threat instead of a measurement, and the crowd began to notice.

**繁中** 元帥帶著全新羊皮紙上的命令抵達，指控瑟拉破壞帝國慶典，要求日落前拘捕她。她大聲讀出罪名，免得日後有人捏造不同說法。「我的預言有有限的代價，」她告訴他，「每次凝視未來，我都會失去一段記憶。若能不再失去記憶就阻止災難，我就會這麼做。」多里安元帥罵她不忠。塔林質問他的工程師為何忽視斷裂銅錨，元帥只用威脅代替測量，群眾也開始注意到。

**EN** The bridge closed one hour before the parade. When the returning soldiers arrived, they grumbled but followed the temporary footpath. At the precise moment Sera had seen in her vision, the central arch dropped into the river. No one was standing on it. The crowd went silent; then the same people who had called her a traitor began shouting that she had saved them. Sera wanted neither verdict. She looked at the repair papers, at the missing signature, and at the river carrying away the stone. A failure had happened and a tragedy had not. Somewhere between those facts lay a future the marshal had not wanted her to see.

**繁中** 遊行前一小時，大橋封閉。返城士兵雖抱怨，仍沿臨時步道前進。就在瑟拉預言的精確時刻，中央橋拱落入河中，上面一個人都沒有。群眾沉默片刻，原本罵她叛徒的人接著高喊她救了大家。瑟拉不想接受任一種判決。她看著維修文件、消失的簽名，以及河水帶走的石塊。失敗確實發生，悲劇卻沒有發生。這兩件事之間，藏著元帥不願讓她看見的未來。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. As troops celebrate a promised victory, Sera sees their triumphal bridge collapse under thousands of returning civilians.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 軍隊慶祝預期勝利時，瑟拉卻看見迎接歸民的凱旋橋在數千人腳下倒塌。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. She prevents the crossing, only to be accused of sabotaging a victory she never predicted.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 她阻止人群過橋，卻被指控破壞一場自己從未預言的勝利。


## CHAPTER 02｜A Prophecy Has No Crown｜預言沒有皇冠

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：858**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Marshal Dorian demands a forecast of the emperor’s next battle, but Sera sees only burning hospitals and abandoned fields. Sera asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。多里安元帥要求預測下一場戰役，瑟拉卻只看見燃燒的醫院和荒廢的田地。瑟拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Sera could witness the worst plausible branch only under the conditions shown to her. A vision could change when those conditions changed, and every look ahead took one ordinary memory she could never recover by magic.

**繁中** 瑟拉只能看見特定條件下最糟但可能發生的分支。條件改變，預言也會改變，而每次窺視未來都會奪走一段無法靠魔法取回的普通記憶。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Sera asked Tarin not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑟拉請塔林不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An aboriginal elder explained the old names of the river bends before any court translator offered an interpretation. Disputes abound when a public notice contains only the palace language and excludes the others. Nessa tried to align the three handwritten headings without altering the irregular spacing beneath them. Keir recorded the altitude of the ridge because spring floods could isolate the crossing below. The magistrate concealed his annoyance when Iva asked him to repeat a legal question slowly. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一位原住民族長者在宮廷譯員解說前，先說明河灣的舊地名。 公告只有宮廷語而排除其他語言時，爭端便大量出現。 妮莎試圖對齊三份手寫標題，但不改動其下不規則的間距。 凱爾記下山脊海拔，因為春洪可能切斷下方渡口。 伊娃請地方法官慢慢重述法律問題時，他掩飾住自己的不耐。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. An official report transforms her warning into a recommendation for an early invasion. Sera watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Tarin recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。官方報告竟把她的警告改寫成先發制人的侵略建議。瑟拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。塔林公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Iva gave a blunt answer: her neighbors had agreed to store grain, never to lose their homes. Sera tried to align their route with the royal charts, but the marked crossing had been destroyed months earlier. Maelin would not discard a damaged draft until both sides had inspected its surviving lines. The river guard received a medical discharge note but still had to answer questions about his shift. Their endeavor was to restore a shared crossing, not to replace one ruler with another. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 伊娃直截了當地回答：鄰居同意保管糧食，從未同意失去家園。 瑟拉試著讓路線與王室地圖對齊，卻發現標示的渡口早在數月前就毀了。 瑟拉不願在雙方檢查殘存字句前丟棄破損草稿。 河岸衛兵拿到出院證明後，仍須回答有關值班的問題。 他們努力的是恢復共用渡口，而非以另一位統治者取代現任者。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Sera insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Tarin drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑟拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。塔林畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A missing customs number became the first hurdle for families seeking to recover their grain. Keir changed his posture when the old survey poles no longer matched the new boundary sketch. The interpreter demanded precision in the word for custody because a careless synonym could transfer ownership. Nessa insisted on a rigorous comparison of every stamped page before anyone announced fraud. Families waited on a stone terrace overlooking the water while the magistrate prepared his order. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 遺失的海關編號成為家庭領回糧食的第一道障礙。 舊測量桿與新邊界圖不符時，凱爾改變站姿仔細查看。 譯員要求精確使用「保管」一詞，因為草率的同義詞可能轉移所有權。 妮莎堅持先嚴格比對每張蓋印頁面，任何人才能宣布有偽造嫌疑。 家人們在俯瞰河水的石露臺等候地方法官起草命令。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The commander feared an uprising if hungry villagers were again told that their names did not matter. Volunteers offered to usher elderly witnesses toward the chamber without choosing their answers for them. The versatile survey cart carried measuring poles, winter blankets, and a box of public notices. The local council chose not to veto the temporary food route merely because the palace disliked it. A widow spoke about the fee she had paid twice for the same ferry passage. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 指揮官擔心若再次告訴挨餓的村民其姓名無關緊要，將引發起義。 志工引導年長證人走向聽證室，但不替他們決定答案。 多用途測量車載著測量桿、冬毯與一箱公開公告。 地方議會沒有只因王宮不喜歡就否決臨時糧食路線。 一名寡婦談起她為同一趟渡船被收取兩次的費用。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Sera passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Tarin paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑟拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。塔林停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

| L6-0254 | `discard` | v./n. | `discard` | 丟棄、排除 | Maelin would not discard a damaged draft until both sides had inspected its surviving lines. |
| L6-0255 | `discharge` | n./v. | `discharge` | 出院、解除（醫療照護） | The river guard received a medical discharge note but still had to answer questions about his shift. |
| L6-0302 | `endeavor` | n./v. | `endeavor` | 努力 | Their endeavor was to restore a shared crossing, not to replace one ruler with another. |
| L6-0428 | `hurdle` | n./v. | `hurdle` | 障礙 | A missing customs number became the first hurdle for families seeking to recover their grain. |
| L6-0682 | `posture` | n./v. | `posture` | 姿態 | Keir changed his posture when the old survey poles no longer matched the new boundary sketch. |
| L6-0688 | `precision` | n. | `precision` | 精確度 | The interpreter demanded precision in the word for custody because a careless synonym could transfer ownership. |

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Marshal Dorian demands a forecast of the emperor’s next battle, but Sera sees only burning hospitals and abandoned fields.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 多里安元帥要求預測下一場戰役，瑟拉卻只看見燃燒的醫院和荒廢的田地。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. An official report transforms her warning into a recommendation for an early invasion.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 官方報告竟把她的警告改寫成先發制人的侵略建議。


## CHAPTER 03｜The Historian Who Asked Why｜問為什麼的史官

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：895**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Court historian Tarin compares three of Sera’s old visions with what actually occurred in the affected villages. Sera checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。宮廷史官塔林比較瑟拉過去三次預言與受影響村莊的實況。瑟拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Sera could witness the worst plausible branch only under the conditions shown to her. A vision could change when those conditions changed, and every look ahead took one ordinary memory she could never recover by magic.

**繁中** 瑟拉只能看見特定條件下最糟但可能發生的分支。條件改變，預言也會改變，而每次窺視未來都會奪走一段無法靠魔法取回的普通記憶。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Sera listened longer than the schedule allowed. Tarin reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑟拉聽得比議程允許的更久。塔林提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An advisory plaque still praised the Empire's conquest of the eastern frontier, but most houses were empty. The hill envoy rejected the word conquest because the agreement described cooperation rather than military victory. Vask called the missing binding a conspiracy invented by people who wanted to embarrass the crown. The innkeeper served cucumber salad while both delegations waited for the witness room to open. An elderly scribe looked feeble, yet he remembered exactly where the archive chest had stood. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 告示牌仍讚揚帝國征服東部邊境，但大多數房屋已空無一人。 山地使者反對使用「征服」，因為協議寫的是合作而非軍事勝利。 瓦斯克把遺失裝訂線稱為企圖羞辱王室的人編造的陰謀。 兩方代表等待證人室開門時，旅店主人端上小黃瓜沙拉。 一名年老書記看似虛弱，卻清楚記得檔案箱原本的位置。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. In each case, a simple repair prevented the catastrophe without a single military order. Sera read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Tarin did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。每一次都是簡單修繕阻止災難，根本不需要軍令。瑟拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。塔林沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A firecracker exploded outside the gate, and the officers checked whether it was a signal or prank. The printer began to fume when investigators asked for the invoice he had already surrendered. Keir wiped grease from a cargo wheel to read the scratched route number beneath it. The preservation order could lessen the risk of destruction but could not determine who had altered the text. A river liner had carried the chest to the island station before the last treaty ceremony. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 門外一枚爆竹炸響，官員檢查究竟是暗號還是惡作劇。 調查員再次索取已上交的發票時，印刷工開始氣憤。 凱爾擦去貨輪上的油脂，讀取下面刻出的路線編號。 保全令能降低文件遭毀的風險，卻不能判定誰改過文字。 一艘河上客輪在最後一次條約儀式前把箱子送往島站。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Marshal Dorian demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Sera replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當多里安元帥要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑟拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The orphanage kept a teaching copy of the river alphabet in its small reading room. An old plague notice proved that the village had used the crossing long before the disputed decree. The recreational sailing club had logged the unusual midnight delivery in its harbor notebook. An angry official called the river protesters savage, and Maelin demanded that the insult be removed from the record. Nessa found a small shrub growing beside the wall where the binding thread had been dropped. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 孤兒院在小閱覽室保存了一份河語字母教學本。 一張舊瘟疫公告證明該村在爭議命令之前很久便使用渡口。 休閒帆船社在港口筆記本記下異常的午夜送貨。 一名憤怒官員稱河民抗議者野蠻，瑟拉要求從紀錄中刪除侮辱用語。 妮莎在裝訂線掉落的牆邊發現一叢小灌木。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The interpreter explained that local slang could not be substituted for a defined legal term. The hill witnesses hoped the evidence would surpass the palace's confident but unsupported account. Three carved stones symbolize the agreement to share the river rather than divide it by force. A textile merchant produced a dated receipt for cloth delivered to the treaty ceremony. A vaccine shipment had passed the same checkpoint, showing that the route served ordinary medical needs. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 譯員解釋，地方俚語不能取代定義明確的法律用語。 山地證人希望證據能勝過王宮自信卻缺乏支持的說法。 三塊雕石象徵共同使用河流，而不是武力瓜分。 一名紡織商拿出條約儀式時交貨的布匹日期收據。 一批疫苗也曾通過同一檢查站，顯示路線服務一般醫療需求。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Sera resisted the urge to call it proof. Tarin opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑟拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。塔林翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Court historian Tarin compares three of Sera’s old visions with what actually occurred in the affected villages.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 宮廷史官塔林比較瑟拉過去三次預言與受影響村莊的實況。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. In each case, a simple repair prevented the catastrophe without a single military order.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 每一次都是簡單修繕阻止災難，根本不需要軍令。


## CHAPTER 04｜The Price of Looking｜凝視未來的代價

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：853**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Sera reveals that every vision removes one ordinary memory of her own, leaving her unsure which childhood stories are real. Sera knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。瑟拉坦白每次預言都會奪走一段平凡記憶，使她逐漸分不清童年的真偽。瑟拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Tarin arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Sera could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 塔林帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑟拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Workers used bleach to clean the boardinghouse corridor, but Nessa forbade cleaning the evidence cabinet. The judges demanded a comprehensive visitor list rather than a collection of rumors about the witnesses. The landlady appeared credible because her account matched the dated kitchen purchase book. Iva thanked the guards for their dedication to keeping witnesses safe without controlling what they said. A torn ribbon lay inside the dresser beside the room where the visiting scribe had slept. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 工人用漂白劑清潔寄宿屋走廊，妮莎卻禁止清洗證物櫃。 法官要求完整訪客名單，而非收集關於證人的流言。 房東太太的說法與附日期的廚房採購簿相符，因此顯得可信。 伊娃感謝衛兵盡心保護證人安全，而不干涉證言內容。 那名訪客書記睡過的房間旁，梳妝檯內放著一段破緞帶。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. Tarin promises to record what she chooses to remember, not to force her to keep seeing disasters. For a long moment no one spoke. Sera understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Tarin broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。塔林答應記下她自願保留的記憶，不會逼她持續看見災難。一時之間無人開口。瑟拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。塔林打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Maelin's familiarity with the novel made her suspicious, but she refused to present it as proof. The palace hierarchy placed interpreters below ministers, although their errors could affect entire villages. The landlady recorded the visitor's time of departure without inventing a reason for the late hour. A lush garden behind the house gave frightened children somewhere quiet to sit. The villagers still mourn the ferryman lost during last year's unsafe winter crossing. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 瑟拉熟悉原著而起疑，卻拒絕把這種熟悉當作證據。 王宮階層把譯員置於大臣之下，他們的錯誤卻可能影響整個村莊。 房東太太只記下訪客離開的時間，沒有猜造他深夜離去的理由。 屋後繁茂的花園讓受驚的孩子有安靜的地方可坐。 村民仍哀悼去年冬季危險渡口事故中失去的渡船夫。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Sera had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Tarin proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑟拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。塔林提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The teacher hoped a fair treaty would nurture trust among children speaking different languages. After heavy rain, water began to overflow the stone basin outside the witness house. Maelin happened to overhear a clerk argue about an order to remove margin notes. An engineer's measurements could refute the claim that the wall had never leaked. A seagull circled above the harbor where the archive chest had been unloaded. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 老師希望公平條約能培養使用不同語言的孩子間的信任。 大雨後，水開始漫出證人屋外的石盆。 瑟拉偶然聽見一名書記爭論刪除頁邊註記的命令。 工程師的測量可以反駁牆壁從未漏水的說法。 一隻海鷗在卸下檔案箱的港口上方盤旋。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The school superintendent offered a neutral classroom for the next comparison meeting. A distant symphony drifted from the town hall while the witnesses checked their statements. The infirmary nurse placed a thermometer beside an ill courier and offered him rest before questioning. Public turmoil increased when officials announced that the border had already been decided. A wholesome supper helped the children settle before the adults returned to their difficult discussions. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 學校督學提供一間中立教室供下次比對會議使用。 證人核對證詞時，遠處市政廳傳來交響樂聲。 醫務室護士把溫度計放在生病信差旁，讓他先休息再接受詢問。 官員宣稱邊界已經決定後，公共動盪加劇。 健康而營養的晚餐讓孩子安定下來，大人才繼續艱難討論。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Sera returned to a cracked prophecy lens. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Tarin remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the imperial observatory waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑟拉再次看向一枚裂開的預言透鏡。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。塔林守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的帝國觀星臺等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. Sera reveals that every vision removes one ordinary memory of her own, leaving her unsure which childhood stories are real.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 瑟拉坦白每次預言都會奪走一段平凡記憶，使她逐漸分不清童年的真偽。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Tarin promises to record what she chooses to remember, not to force her to keep seeing disasters.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 塔林答應記下她自願保留的記憶，不會逼她持續看見災難。


## CHAPTER 05｜The Grain Ship That Sank Twice｜沉沒兩次的糧船

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：846**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. A grain ship appears in Sera’s vision sinking at dawn, but an independent captain changes its course. Sera had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the imperial observatory, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。瑟拉看見糧船黎明沉沒，獨立船長卻自作主張改變航線。瑟拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在帝國觀星臺，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Sera asked Tarin to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。瑟拉請塔林說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Several students quietly began to rejoice; others feared that the academy would be closed for allowing dissent. Vask rejected the accusation that his office had changed the treaty for private gain. At the banquet, every guest received a copy of the disputed clause in three languages. A farmer explained that fertilizer purchased for spring planting was now locked inside a seized warehouse. The coastal fishery had lost buyers because the new checkpoint charged an unauthorized fee. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 幾名學生悄悄歡欣，其他人則害怕學院因容許異議而被關閉。 瓦斯克否認其辦公室為私利修改條約的指控。 宴會上每位賓客都拿到三種語言並列的爭議條款。 農民解釋，為春耕購買的肥料如今被鎖在遭扣押的倉庫。 沿岸漁業因新檢查站收取未授權費用而失去買家。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. The ship survives, proving a failed future is a warning rather than a fixed sentence. Sera felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。糧船成功靠岸，證明失敗的未來是警訊，不是既定判決。瑟拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Keir reported a fracture in the bridge beam that could endanger the next grain convoy. Dock workers used a safety harness while repairing the warehouse roof before the rain. An archivist wore headphones to review a preserved reading of the treaty ceremony. The emergency grain intake had doubled, but the market had no public record of distribution. The maiden voyage of a new ferry brought delegates who had never met the palace officers. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 凱爾報告橋梁橫樑有裂縫，可能危及下一支運糧隊。 碼頭工人在雨前修理倉庫屋頂時使用安全吊帶。 檔案員戴著耳機聆聽保存的條約儀式宣讀錄音。 緊急糧食收取量倍增，市場卻沒有公開分配紀錄。 新渡船的首航載來從未見過宮廷官員的代表。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Marshal Dorian and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Sera rejected both. The issue was whether fear of disaster justifies the empire’s wars, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住多里安元帥的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑟拉兩者都拒絕。核心是對災難的恐懼是否足以合理化帝國發動戰爭，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: An offshore storm forced the banquet's planned river parade to be cancelled. A prospective buyer was told that the grain's ownership remained under legal review. The Assembly demanded a referendum only after residents had received translations they could actually read. Nessa asked the clerks to refine the public table without concealing the original spelling. Families could rejoice at the temporary food order without pretending the land dispute was over. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 近海風暴迫使宴會預定的河上遊行取消。 一名潛在買家被告知，糧食所有權仍在司法審理中。 議會要求先讓居民讀懂譯文，再舉行公民投票。 妮莎請書記改善公開對照表，但不可隱藏原始拼法。 家人可以為臨時配糧令欣喜，無須假裝土地爭議已結束。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Some fishers began to resent the palace spokesman for describing their losses as trivial. A serene moment followed when the three delegations read the same sentence aloud together. The temple speaker gave a sermon about promises but refused to decide what the treaty legally meant. The guests had to shuffle between the hall and the archive to inspect the authenticated seals. A tenant farmer testified that the governor had collected payment on land he did not own. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 部分漁民開始怨恨王宮發言人，因為他把損失說得微不足道。 三方代表一起朗讀同一句話後，現場出現短暫寧靜。 寺院講者發表關於承諾的講道，卻拒絕替條約的法律意思下定論。 賓客必須在宴會廳與檔案室間緩步移動，查看認證印章。 一名佃農作證說，總督在不屬於他的土地上收取費用。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Sera had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Tarin wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Marshal Dorian. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑟拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。塔林把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起多里安元帥注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. A grain ship appears in Sera’s vision sinking at dawn, but an independent captain changes its course.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瑟拉看見糧船黎明沉沒，獨立船長卻自作主張改變航線。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The ship survives, proving a failed future is a warning rather than a fixed sentence.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 糧船成功靠岸，證明失敗的未來是警訊，不是既定判決。


## CHAPTER 06｜The Chamber of Red Maps｜紅色地圖室

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：845**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Sera discovers a secret chamber where generals mark her catastrophic visions as borders to be conquered. Sera saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。瑟拉發現密室，將軍把她預見的災難當作要征服的邊界畫在圖上。瑟拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Sera could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Tarin suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，瑟拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。塔林提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The city council could allege misconduct, but Maelin asked it to identify the specific page and signature. People had tried to beautify the hall with flowers for the midsummer festival, but protest notices covered the gates. Maelin traced the caption to a committee meeting where no qualified river interpreter had been present. The hearing reached its climax when a young cartographer refused to repeat an unverified claim. Nessa used a special lamp to extract faint writing from beneath the later ink without damaging the sheet. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 市議會可以指控不當行為，但瑟拉要求指出具體頁碼與簽名。 人們原本用花朵美化大廳，迎接仲夏節，如今大門卻貼滿抗議公告。 瑟拉查出圖說來自一場沒有合格河語口譯員出席的委員會議。 當年輕製圖員拒絕重複未經證實的說法時，聽證達到高潮。 妮莎利用特殊燈具辨認後加墨水下的淡字，沒有損壞紙張。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. Several disaster sites are on land that has never threatened the empire. Tarin asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Sera agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。數個災難地點位於從未威脅帝國的土地。塔林詢問是否可能只是出錯，瑟拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: An extracurricular mapmaking club had kept an earlier classroom sketch of the bridge. The village inn was hospitable to visiting observers even when they disagreed with its owners. A jolly old driver offered tea while the investigators compared delivery times. Farm workers had to mow a narrow path before surveyors could reach the buried marker. The clerk with a thick mustache remembered carrying the map tube into the committee room. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一個課外製圖社團保存了更早的教室橋梁草圖。 村莊旅店熱情接待訪客觀察員，即使他們與店主意見不合。 一位開朗的老車夫在調查員比對送貨時間時端來茶。 測量員到達埋沒的界標前，農工必須先割出一條狹窄小路。 留著濃鬍子的書記記得曾把地圖筒帶進委員會議室。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Sera insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Tarin drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑟拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。塔林畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Maelin would not nag the witness into an answer when he honestly could not recall the hour. The riverside shelter became an oasis for displaced families during the dispute. The sealing wax weighed less than an ounce, yet its broken edge offered a useful comparison. The printing contractor operated on the outskirts of the capital, beyond the usual patrol route. The parliament record showed the emergency border powers had never been approved in full. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 證人真心想不起時間時，瑟拉不願反覆催逼他回答。 爭端期間，河邊避難所成了流離失所家庭的綠洲。 封蠟不到一盎司重，破損邊緣卻提供有用的比對依據。 印刷承包商在首都郊區營業，位於一般巡邏路線之外。 議會紀錄顯示，緊急邊界權力從未獲得完整核准。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A ruthless collector threatened to seize grain even after the suspension order arrived. A sculptor confirmed that the stone boundary marker had been moved only recently. Maelin preferred the simplicity of a dated original to the grandeur of an unsupported proclamation. A simultaneous release of both chambers was therefore forbidden until the second inspection. Public confidence fell into a slump when the palace refused to explain the disputed caption. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 即使暫停令已送達，一名無情的徵收員仍威脅扣押糧食。 一名雕刻師確認，石製界標是最近才被移動的。 相較缺乏證據的華麗宣告，瑟拉更重視附日期原件的簡明。 因此，在第二次檢查以前，兩個艙室不得同時放能。 王宮拒絕解釋爭議圖說，公眾信心因此陷入低潮。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Sera passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Tarin paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑟拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。塔林停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Sera discovers a secret chamber where generals mark her catastrophic visions as borders to be conquered.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 瑟拉發現密室，將軍把她預見的災難當作要征服的邊界畫在圖上。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. Several disaster sites are on land that has never threatened the empire.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 數個災難地點位於從未威脅帝國的土地。


## CHAPTER 07｜An Unwritten Forecast｜未寫出的預報

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：868**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Sera refused to begin with the official conclusion. Tarin asks Sera to describe what she did not see, and they identify a dozen peaceful possibilities excluded from the archive. In the imperial observatory, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。瑟拉拒絕從官方結論開始。塔林請瑟拉說明自己沒看見什麼，兩人找出檔案刻意排除的十幾種和平可能。在帝國觀星臺，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Sera asked Tarin not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑟拉請塔林不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The survey covered an acre of wet meadow that the palace pamphlet called newly acquired crown land. A bachelor who had lived beside the meadow all his life showed Keir the oldest boundary stone. The village boxing club had stored its equipment in a barn now claimed by royal collectors. The council announced a disciplinary review for the officer who had ignored the suspension order. An emigrant returning home discovered that the revised map no longer showed her family's grazing route. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 測量涵蓋一英畝潮濕草地，王宮小冊子稱之為新取得的王室土地。 一名終身住在草地旁的單身漢向凱爾指出最古老的界石。 村裡的拳擊社曾把器材存放在一間如今遭王室徵收員主張所有權的穀倉。 議會宣布對無視暫停令的官員進行紀律審查。 一名返鄉移民發現新版地圖不再顯示家族放牧路線。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. The archivist refuses to enter the omissions, fearing they will be treated as treason. Sera watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Tarin recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。檔案官拒絕登錄這些缺漏，擔心被當成叛國。瑟拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。塔林公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Maelin refused to equate a temporary lease with permanent ownership simply because one English verb sounded convenient. The young witness began to falter when Vask's lawyer asked three questions at once. Farmers argued that the fertility of the floodplain depended on seasonal access to both banks. Iva would not forsake her community's claim merely to secure a private appointment with the governor. A gay teacher asked the committee to use the names families chose, not those assigned in outdated registers. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 瑟拉拒絕只因一個英語動詞方便，就把暫時租賃等同永久所有權。 瓦斯克律師一次提出三個問題時，年輕證人開始結巴猶豫。 農民指出，沖積平原的土壤肥力仰賴兩岸季節性通行。 伊娃不願只為得到總督私下接見就放棄社區權利主張。 一位男同志教師要求委員會使用家庭自己選的名字，而非過時登記所指定的名字。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Marshal Dorian demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Sera replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當多里安元帥要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑟拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Gloom spread through the room when the court announced that compensation would require another hearing. The old intersection of farm roads appeared on all three authenticated survey maps. A landslide delayed the inspection, so Keir refused to send inexperienced workers uphill. The local school proposed a food program to nourish children while grain ownership remained unresolved. The orthodox court scholars disliked the river dialect but admitted its grammar was legally relevant. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 法庭宣布補償還需另開聽證時，陰鬱氣氛籠罩全場。 那處古老農路交叉口在三份經認證的測量圖上都存在。 山崩延誤檢查，凱爾因此拒絕派缺乏經驗的工人上山。 當糧食所有權仍未解決時，地方學校提出供餐計畫滋養孩童。 正統宮廷學者不喜歡河語方言，卻承認其文法具有法律相關性。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A prohibition against night crossings remained in effect only where bridge damage had been independently confirmed. The provisional route notice would expire after seven days unless a new safety review justified renewal. The vote was unanimous, yet the council could not erase an independent court order. The governor's vanity had led him to display a larger map than anyone could read closely. A woodpecker struck a hollow tree beside the stone that marked the meadow's western edge. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 夜間禁渡只在橋梁損傷經獨立確認的地方繼續生效。 暫行路線公告七天後失效，除非新的安全審查證明需要延長。 表決結果一致，議會卻無法抹除獨立法庭的命令。 總督的虛榮使他展示了一張大得無法近看細讀的地圖。 一隻啄木鳥敲擊草地西緣界石旁的空心樹。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Sera resisted the urge to call it proof. Tarin opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑟拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。塔林翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

| L6-0957 | `vanity` | n. | `vanity` | 虛榮 | The governor's vanity had led him to display a larger map than anyone could read closely. |
| L6-0995 | `woodpecker` | n. | `woodpecker` | 啄木鳥 | A woodpecker struck a hollow tree beside the stone that marked the meadow's western edge. |

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Tarin asks Sera to describe what she did not see, and they identify a dozen peaceful possibilities excluded from the archive.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 塔林請瑟拉說明自己沒看見什麼，兩人找出檔案刻意排除的十幾種和平可能。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The archivist refuses to enter the omissions, fearing they will be treated as treason.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 檔案官拒絕登錄這些缺漏，擔心被當成叛國。


## CHAPTER 08｜A Small Failure Saved｜一場被救下的小失敗

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：859**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Sera publicly predicts a market fire, and stall owners move their oil before the spark appears. Sera asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。瑟拉公開預告市集失火，攤商在火花出現前把油料移開。瑟拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Sera listened longer than the schedule allowed. Tarin reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑟拉聽得比議程允許的更久。塔林提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The storage log showed an accumulation of sealed bundles beneath the classroom floor over several years. A blond clerk named Ruen carried a beverage to a retired teacher who would testify about missing children. A blond teacher carefully unfolded the school copy without touching its inked surface. Two appointed experts had to certify the age of the paper before the court could rely on it. Nessa requested written clearance to photograph the school copy and leave the original in place. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 儲藏簿顯示，數年間教室地板下累積了多捆封存文件。 一名金髮書記魯恩端飲料給退休教師，她將為失蹤的孩子作證。 一名金髮老師小心展開學校副本，沒有碰觸墨跡表面。 法院採信前，必須有兩名指定專家證明紙張年代。 妮莎申請書面許可拍攝學校副本，原件則留在原處。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. The resulting non-event earns no official praise, exposing how the court rewards disasters more than prevention. Sera read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Tarin did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。火災沒有發生，宮廷卻不予讚賞，暴露朝廷獎勵災難勝過預防。瑟拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。塔林沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The clerk could deduct transport expenses from the budget only with receipts signed by both custodians. Maelin realized the copied warning was destined to matter more than the elegant ceremonial title. The survey team had to exert care when lifting the old storage chest from damp floorboards. The villagers called the teacher's quiet refusal to surrender the original heroic, though she asked for no praise. A retired lieutenant helped protect the school without interfering with the investigators' authority. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 書記只有拿到雙方保管者簽署的收據，才能從預算扣除運費。 瑟拉發現副本裡的警告注定比華麗的儀式標題更重要。 測量團隊從潮濕地板抬起舊儲藏箱時必須格外用力且小心。 村民稱老師拒絕交出原件的沉默堅持很英勇，她卻不求讚美。 一名退役中尉協助保護學校，沒有干涉調查權限。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Sera had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Tarin proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑟拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。塔林提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Every literate child in the school could read the disputed river sentence more accurately than the official pamphlet. A lotus flower had been pressed between the pages as a marker for the river passage. The marginal note explained why the river verb described custody rather than ownership. The seals showed an overlap of ink from two different periods, prompting further laboratory tests. A provincial inspector confirmed that the classroom register had not left the district for ten years. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 學校裡每個識字的孩子都能比官方小冊子更準確讀懂爭議河語句。 一朵蓮花被壓在書頁間，作為河流條款的書籤。 頁邊註記解釋為何河語動詞指保管而非所有權。 印章顯示兩個年代的墨跡重疊，因此需要進一步化驗。 一名地方督察確認，教室登記冊十年間未離開該區。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The interpreter's professional qualification did not excuse her from explaining an omitted warning. Parents offered refreshments after the experts completed their examination of the preserved copy. Nessa promised to retrieve a second copy only after receiving permission from the Hill Council. The provincial court stood beside a swamp where lotus flowers opened at dawn. The children learned that a translator's vocation involved responsibility to readers, not blind loyalty to rulers. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 翻譯官的專業資格不能免除她解釋警語為何被省略的責任。 專家完成保存副本的檢查後，家長提供茶點。 妮莎承諾取得山地議會許可後才取回第二份副本。 省級法庭位於沼澤旁，黎明時蓮花盛開。 孩子們學到翻譯家的職志是對讀者負責，而非盲目效忠統治者。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Sera returned to a cracked prophecy lens. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Tarin remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the imperial observatory waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑟拉再次看向一枚裂開的預言透鏡。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。塔林守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的帝國觀星臺等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Sera publicly predicts a market fire, and stall owners move their oil before the spark appears.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瑟拉公開預告市集失火，攤商在火花出現前把油料移開。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The resulting non-event earns no official praise, exposing how the court rewards disasters more than prevention.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 火災沒有發生，宮廷卻不予讚賞，暴露朝廷獎勵災難勝過預防。


## CHAPTER 09｜The General's Empty Witness｜將軍的空白證人

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：861**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. A witness to the border incident is listed as dead in two different years, yet his daughter says he is alive. Sera checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。邊境事件證人被記載在兩個不同年份死亡，女兒卻表示他還活著。瑟拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Tarin arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Sera could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 塔林帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑟拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Maelin feared that careless accusations would alienate the Hill Council before the evidence could be examined. Nessa examined a blot of blue ink beside the disputed verb on the hill copy. The innkeeper added chili to the stew served after the long mountain journey. A witness list supplied continuity between the first treaty meeting and the later public reading. The cumulative weight of small dated records became harder for the palace to dismiss. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 瑟拉擔心草率指控會在查驗證據前疏遠山地議會。 妮莎檢查山地副本爭議動詞旁的一滴藍色墨漬。 旅店主人在長途山行後端上的燉菜裡加入辣椒。 證人名單使首次條約會議與後來公開宣讀之間的脈絡得以連續。 許多附日期的小紀錄累積起來的證據分量，使王宮更難忽視。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. Sera suspects that the court has been editing the events that trigger her visions. For a long moment no one spoke. Sera understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Tarin broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。瑟拉懷疑宮廷一直修改引發她預言的事件。一時之間無人開口。瑟拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。塔林打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Anika demanded a decisive answer about whether local interpreters had reviewed the printed summary. A witness's accent was not dispensable merely because the court clerk found it inconvenient. Jasmine grew near the inn window where the delegates reviewed their copied documents. The hill lodge offered both sides equal rooms and refused to charge the river guests more. A red maple leaf marked the treaty page the old translator had asked his daughter to remember. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 安妮卡要求明確回答：地方譯員是否審查過印製摘要。 證人的口音不能只因法庭書記覺得麻煩就被當作可有可無。 代表審閱抄件的旅店窗邊長著茉莉花。 山區旅舍提供雙方同樣房間，拒絕向河民多收費。 一片紅楓葉標記著老翻譯員請女兒記住的條約頁面。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Marshal Dorian and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Sera rejected both. The issue was whether fear of disaster justifies the empire’s wars, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住多里安元帥的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑟拉兩者都拒絕。核心是對災難的恐懼是否足以合理化帝國發動戰爭，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The mistress of the house showed a dated guest book recording the translator's last visit. Local volunteers agreed to mobilize carts for a return journey before the mountain snow arrived. Maelin began to muse about how many borders had been changed by ordinary-looking words. Anika's persistence persuaded a reluctant clerk to release the original meeting attendance sheet. A socialist printer joined the public debate, arguing that agreements must be legible to working families. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 女主人出示附日期的訪客簿，記錄老譯員最後一次來訪。 當地志工同意在山雪來臨前動員馬車協助返程。 瑟拉開始沉思，究竟有多少邊界是被看似普通的字詞改變。 安妮卡的堅持讓猶豫的書記交出原始會議出席表。 一名社會主義印刷工加入公共辯論，認為協議必須讓勞動家庭讀得懂。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Delegates could socialize after the meeting, but they were forbidden to negotiate secret changes to the record. The access log covered a span of seven hours, with one unexplained gap near midnight. The final trek down the slope took longer because the team carried fragile copies in sealed boxes. A vigorous debate followed the discovery of the deceased translator's mark beside the clause. The waterproof wrapping had protected the hill copy through three seasons of heavy rain. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 代表可在會後交際，卻禁止私下協商更改紀錄。 門禁紀錄涵蓋七小時，其中午夜附近有一段無法解釋的空白。 下坡的最後一段跋涉較久，因為團隊以封箱搬運脆弱副本。 發現已故譯員在條款旁的記號後，現場展開激烈辯論。 防水包裝讓山地副本挺過三季豪雨。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Sera had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Tarin wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Marshal Dorian. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑟拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。塔林把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起多里安元帥注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. A witness to the border incident is listed as dead in two different years, yet his daughter says he is alive.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 邊境事件證人被記載在兩個不同年份死亡，女兒卻表示他還活著。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. Sera suspects that the court has been editing the events that trigger her visions.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 瑟拉懷疑宮廷一直修改引發她預言的事件。


## CHAPTER 10｜The Worst Future Was Selected｜最糟的未來被挑出來

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：876**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. At the war council, Tarin proves the empire publishes only the most frightening of many possible visions. Sera knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。在軍事議會上，塔林證明帝國只公布眾多可能未來中最恐怖的一個。瑟拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Tarin carried a locked box into the war council and set it before the emperor. Inside were twelve versions of the same forecast: in one a bridge collapsed, in another the river changed course, and in seven the war never began. Only the bloodiest vision had reached the public. Sera checked the ink, the dates, and the seal; every version was genuine. Marshal Dorian called the others irrelevant because they did not frighten anyone into action. Sera could suddenly see how her gift had been converted into a weapon. She had never claimed that failure was inevitable. The court had edited possibility into certainty, then ordered thousands to kill in its name. She stood and requested that the entire record be read aloud.

**繁中** 塔林帶著上鎖木盒走進軍事議會，放在皇帝面前。盒內有同一份預報的十二個版本：有的橋倒塌、有的河流改道，另有七個版本裡戰爭根本沒有開始。公開的卻只有最血腥的一種。瑟拉核對墨跡、日期與印璽，所有版本都是真的。多里安元帥稱其他版本毫無意義，因為它們不會嚇得人們採取行動。瑟拉突然明白，自己的天賦如何被變成武器。她從未說失敗不可避免。朝廷卻把可能性剪輯成確定性，再以它之名命令數千人殺戮。她站起身，要求宣讀全部紀錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The chancellor offered a concession that would feed the villages only if they accepted his border claim. Maelin heard a cynical laugh when she asked why hungry families had been given such a choice. The treasury threatened to default on the bridge contract unless disputed grain fees were collected. Winter hunger could deplete the villagers' food stores long before the judges finished their arguments. The fragrance of warm bread drifted into the hearing room from the public kitchen outside. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 宰相提出讓步：村民只有接受他的邊界主張，才能獲得糧食。 瑟拉詢問為何要逼挨餓家庭作這種選擇時，聽見一聲冷嘲。 國庫威脅，若不收取有爭議的糧食費，就會拖欠橋梁合約款項。 法官辯論結束前，冬季飢餓可能早已耗盡村民糧食儲備。 熱麵包的香氣從外面的公共廚房飄進聽證室。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Dorian reveals a locked vault of less disastrous alternatives that he has concealed for years. Sera felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。多里安承認自己多年來把災難較小的替代可能封存在密庫。瑟拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A palace agent tried to intrude on the confidential meeting between Iva and her legal adviser. A young jockey delivered a sealed message after taking the fastest road beside the racetrack. Anika demanded a literal reading of the disputed sentence before the palace offered its interpretation. The temporary food order raised morale among ferry workers who had feared losing their winter wages. The tribunal could prosecute deliberate fraud only after establishing who had authorized the replacement. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 王宮代理人試圖闖入伊娃與法律顧問的保密會面。 一名年輕騎師沿賽馬場旁最快的路送來封口信件。 安妮卡要求先逐字朗讀爭議句，再聽王宮的解釋。 臨時配糧令提高了渡口工人的士氣，他們原本擔心失去冬季工資。 法庭必須確定誰核准替換文件，才能起訴故意詐欺。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Sera insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Tarin drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑟拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。塔林畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: An old mining report mentioned radioactive stone, but Keir confirmed it had no connection to the treaty case. Vask delivered a sharp retort when Maelin said that a convenient translation was not necessarily a lawful one. A mechanic used a screwdriver to repair the hearing room's broken speaking stand before testimony resumed. Each serving of soup was recorded separately so food distribution could not be confused with land settlement. The splendor of the royal hall could not hide the hunger described by witnesses from the river villages. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 舊礦業報告提及放射性石料，凱爾確認它與條約案件無關。 瑟拉說方便的翻譯未必合法時，瓦斯克尖刻地反駁。 技師用螺絲起子修好聽證室損壞的發言台，證詞才得以繼續。 每份湯食單獨記錄，避免配糧與土地和解混為一談。 王室大廳的華麗掩蓋不了河岸村民證詞中的飢餓。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A spontaneous offer from local bakers filled the temporary shelter with bread before nightfall. The boy began to stutter when he saw his stepfather at the back of the hall. The young clerk began to stutter under pressure, so the judge gave him time to answer slowly. The government agreed to subsidize emergency grain transport without demanding a surrender of property rights. A synthetic ribbon on the false archive seal differed from the natural fiber used in the originals. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 當地麵包師自發提供協助，夜幕前把麵包送滿臨時避難所。 少年在庭後看見繼父，說話開始結巴。 年輕書記在壓力下開始口吃，法官讓他慢慢回答。 政府同意補貼緊急運糧，不要求居民放棄財產權。 偽造檔案封印上的合成纖維帶與原件使用的天然纖維不同。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Sera passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Tarin paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑟拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。塔林停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。
