## CHAPTER 11｜The Trial of a Warning｜警告受審

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：831**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Sera is charged with betraying state secrets after she asks citizens to review the unpublished predictions. Sera had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the imperial observatory, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。瑟拉要求人民檢閱未公開預言，因此遭控洩露國家機密。瑟拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在帝國觀星臺，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Sera could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Tarin suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，瑟拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。塔林提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The temperature in the print shop rose above thirty degrees Celsius during the inspection. Friction had worn the old seal plate unevenly, unlike the smooth new impression. A guard dog began to growl when strangers approached the workshop's rear door. Maelin would not induce a confession by promising a pardon the tribunal had never authorized. Several families inhabit the alley beside the workshop, and their accounts of deliveries were recorded separately. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 檢查期間，印刷坊的氣溫升到攝氏三十度以上。 摩擦使舊印版磨損不均，與光滑的新印痕不同。 陌生人靠近工坊後門時，警犬開始低吼。 瑟拉不會藉承諾法庭未核准的赦免來誘導供認。 幾戶家庭住在工坊旁的巷子裡，他們對送貨的說法被分別記錄。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. The charge forces the court to explain why a prediction is treated as military property. Tarin asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Sera agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。控罪反而迫使法庭說明，為何預言竟被當成軍事財產。塔林詢問是否可能只是出錯，瑟拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: It was ironic that the palace demanded perfect language while publishing a summary no one had checked. The workers found a mound of discarded paper behind a wall used for temporary storage. A peacock wandered across the old courtyard as investigators examined the sealed printing plates. The town pianist stopped practicing when the officers began their formal interviews. Nessa used a narrow probe to inspect the edge of the plate without touching the engraved letters. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 王宮要求語言完美，自己卻發行未經核對的摘要，實在諷刺。 工人在臨時儲物牆後發現一堆丟棄的紙張。 調查員檢查封存印版時，一隻孔雀走過舊庭院。 官員開始正式訪談時，鎮上的鋼琴師停止練習。 妮莎用細探針檢查印版邊緣，沒有碰到刻字。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Marshal Dorian demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Sera replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當多里安元帥要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑟拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Each witness received a copy of the notes taken during their respective interviews. A shortsighted minister called the missing word unimportant because the map looked almost unchanged. Maelin tried to simplify the public explanation without removing the legal qualification from the original. Ships crossing the strait depended on accurate winter notices from the same office that printed the treaty. Nessa refused a superficial inspection and measured the spacing between engraved letters. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 每名證人都收到自己那場訪談的筆記副本。 一名目光短淺的大臣稱遺失的字不重要，因為地圖看來幾乎沒變。 瑟拉試圖簡化公開說明，但不刪去原文的法律限定語。 穿越海峽的船隻仰賴同一辦公室發布的正確冬季公告。 妮莎拒絕膚淺檢查，仔細測量刻字間距。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: No officer was allowed to suppress a witness statement merely because it contradicted the palace account. The perfect symmetry of the new seal suggested a different tool from the worn official stamp. A brass token bearing the shop number appeared in the carrier's payment purse. The delegates hoped the three authentic versions could unify the public record without erasing their differences. An urgency notice required investigators to preserve the plates before the workshop reopened. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 任何官員都不得只因證詞與王宮說法相反就壓下證言。 新印記過於對稱，顯示可能使用不同於磨損官印的工具。 信差的付款袋裡出現一枚帶工坊編號的黃銅代幣。 代表希望三份真實版本能統一公開紀錄，同時不抹去差異。 一份緊急通知要求調查員在工坊重開前保全印版。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Sera resisted the urge to call it proof. Tarin opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑟拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。塔林翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Sera is charged with betraying state secrets after she asks citizens to review the unpublished predictions.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瑟拉要求人民檢閱未公開預言，因此遭控洩露國家機密。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The charge forces the court to explain why a prediction is treated as military property.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 控罪反而迫使法庭說明，為何預言竟被當成軍事財產。


## CHAPTER 12｜The Child of the Floodplain｜洪泛地的孩子

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：856**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. A child from the river plain recounts how her village survived an expected flood through ordinary evacuation drills. Sera saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。河漫灘的孩子說出村莊如何靠普通撤離演習撐過預言中的洪水。瑟拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Sera asked Tarin not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑟拉請塔林不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A narrow brook behind the island workshop supplied fresh water to the mechanics. Keir found coral fragments beneath a pier that the old treaty listed as shared shelter. The double fee could deprive boat owners of money needed for winter repairs. The harbor council voted to elevate safety inspection above the argument over ownership. The lease carried an expiration date that the palace summary had quietly omitted. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 島上工坊後的一條小溪替技師提供淡水。 凱爾在一座舊條約列為共用避難所的碼頭下發現珊瑚碎片。 雙重收費可能剝奪船主冬季修船所需的資金。 港務議會投票決定把安全檢查置於所有權爭論之上。 租約有到期日期，王宮摘要卻悄悄省略了它。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Her testimony makes Sera’s vision useful without requiring anyone to surrender power. Sera watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Tarin recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。她的證詞使預言能幫助人，卻不必讓任何人交出權力。瑟拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。塔林公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The old boat builder's eyesight had weakened, so his apprentice read the dates aloud. A hearty meal was prepared for everyone stranded by the storm, including the visiting officials. A rescue bird seemed to hover above the harbor entrance before settling on the roof. The inland authorities had demanded fees for boats using an island landing they did not own. The witnesses' insistence on keeping the original lease prevented a second undocumented substitution. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 老船匠視力衰退，因此由學徒朗讀日期。 為所有因暴風雨受困的人準備了豐盛餐點，包括外來官員。 一隻救援隊飼養的鳥似乎在港口入口上方盤旋，然後停在屋頂。 內陸當局曾向使用其無所有權島岸碼頭的船隻收費。 證人堅持保存原租約，防止第二次未記錄的替換。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Sera had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Tarin proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑟拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。塔林提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Maelin and Keir crossed the strait to inspect the original lease, leaving the legal debate open while dangerous boats were repaired. The legislator asked who had approved the new fee before any storm warning was issued. The majestic cliffs of the island looked unchanged despite the conflict written into the paperwork. Anika used the storm's momentum to persuade rival officers to open a shared shelter. The monarch had signed the original treaty but not the later pamphlet sent to the island. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 瑟拉和凱爾跨海檢查原租約，同時讓法律爭議保持開放，並先修好危險船隻。 議員詢問，誰在風暴警報發布前就核准新費用。 島上雄偉峭壁看來未變，紙上的爭議卻已延燒。 安妮卡利用風暴帶來的迫切感，說服對立官員開放共用避難所。 君主簽署過原始條約，卻未簽署送往島上的後來小冊子。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A boatman's offspring produced the family receipt that recorded the first legal landing fee. The ferry crew reserved petrol for medical trips instead of using it to carry ceremonial guests. The wind began to rotate the loose harbor sign until Keir tied it securely. Maelin warned that the false fee might strangle the island's small repair trade over time. The nurse carried a surgical kit to the storm shelter in case anyone was injured. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 船夫的子女拿出家庭收據，記載最早的合法靠岸費。 渡船員預留汽油給醫療航次，而非運送典禮賓客。 風開始使鬆動的港口標誌旋轉，直到凱爾將它綁牢。 瑟拉警告，不實收費長期可能扼殺島上小型修船業。 護士把外科急救工具帶到風暴避難所，以備有人受傷。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Sera returned to a cracked prophecy lens. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Tarin remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the imperial observatory waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑟拉再次看向一枚裂開的預言透鏡。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。塔林守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的帝國觀星臺等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. A child from the river plain recounts how her village survived an expected flood through ordinary evacuation drills.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 河漫灘的孩子說出村莊如何靠普通撤離演習撐過預言中的洪水。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. Her testimony makes Sera’s vision useful without requiring anyone to surrender power.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 她的證詞使預言能幫助人，卻不必讓任何人交出權力。


## CHAPTER 13｜A Map of What We Can Change｜我們能改變的地圖

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：868**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Sera refused to begin with the official conclusion. Tarin and Sera create a public map showing the conditions that would make each disaster more or less likely. In the imperial observatory, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。瑟拉拒絕從官方結論開始。塔林與瑟拉製作公開地圖，標出哪些條件會使災難更可能或更不可能發生。在帝國觀星臺，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Sera listened longer than the schedule allowed. Tarin reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑟拉聽得比議程允許的更久。塔林提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A page of algebra exercises was tucked inside the dictionary by a student who had borrowed it. The scholar explained that the antonym of temporary was permanent, not compulsory. The River Assembly wanted to conserve the treaty's original wording instead of replacing it with a convenient paraphrase. The librarian was an outgoing woman who kept a discreet inventory of each volume sent for repair. A bandage dressing was changed in the infirmary while the judges continued their linguistic examination. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名借書學生把一頁代數練習夾進辭典。 學者解釋，「暫時」的反義詞是「永久」，不是「強制」。 河民議會希望保存條約原句，而非用方便的改寫取代。 圖書管理員性格外向，卻謹慎記錄每本送修書籍的清單。 法官繼續語言審查時，醫務室更換了病患的敷料。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. The first districts to use it report fewer injuries, but the general calls the map an enemy weapon. Sera read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Tarin did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。首批使用的街區傷者減少，元帥卻稱地圖為敵國武器。瑟拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。塔林沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: At dusk, Maelin read the old marginal note one final time before submitting it. The school's encyclopedia gave a historical example of the river word used for temporary safekeeping. The memory of her own careless signature would haunt Maelin even if the tribunal cleared her of treason. The servant's voice carried no bitterness, only a fatigue born from official indifference. Nessa completed an inventory of every dictionary, draft, and translation note admitted as evidence. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 黃昏時，瑟拉在提交前最後一次閱讀舊頁邊註記。 學校百科全書提供河語該詞表示暫時保管的歷史例句。 即使法庭洗清叛國罪，自己草率簽名的記憶仍會縈繞瑟拉。 侍者沒有怨恨，只有官方冷漠造成的疲憊。 妮莎完成所有列為證據的辭典、草稿和翻譯註記清單。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Marshal Dorian and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Sera rejected both. The issue was whether fear of disaster justifies the empire’s wars, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住多里安元帥的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑟拉兩者都拒絕。核心是對災難的恐懼是否足以合理化帝國發動戰爭，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The lullaby heard from the village window reminded Maelin whom the winter food rule affected. An outgoing teacher volunteered to explain the disputed grammar during the public session. The river witnesses outnumber the palace officials, but the judge insists that evidence is not decided by head count. The outward appearance of the two treaty books was similar, while their inner clauses differed. A court officer had to restrain an angry spectator who rushed toward the translation table. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 村屋窗內傳來的搖籃曲提醒瑟拉，冬糧規則影響的是誰。 一名外向的老師自願在公開場次解釋爭議文法。 河民證人數量多於宮廷官員，但法官堅持證據不能靠人數決定。 兩份條約冊外表相似，內部條文卻不同。 一名法庭官員不得不攔住衝向翻譯桌的憤怒旁聽者。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A sportswoman from the village testified that the crossing fee had prevented children from attending competitions. The council praised sportsmanship when two rival teams agreed to share the safe ferry schedule. A visitor's suitcase contained nothing but clothing and a carefully wrapped dictionary. The judge issued a tentative finding that the word meant custody, subject to expert review. With the grammatical inquiry underway, Maelin turned to the separate question of lost harvests. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一名村裡女運動員作證，渡口費用妨礙孩子參加比賽。 兩支敵對隊伍同意共用安全渡船時刻，議會讚賞他們的運動精神。 訪客的行李箱裡只有衣物和一本小心包裹的辭典。 法官提出暫時認定：該詞意為保管，但仍須專家複查。 文法調查進行中，瑟拉轉向失去收成的獨立問題。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Sera had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Tarin wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Marshal Dorian. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑟拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。塔林把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起多里安元帥注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Tarin and Sera create a public map showing the conditions that would make each disaster more or less likely.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 塔林與瑟拉製作公開地圖，標出哪些條件會使災難更可能或更不可能發生。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The first districts to use it report fewer injuries, but the general calls the map an enemy weapon.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 首批使用的街區傷者減少，元帥卻稱地圖為敵國武器。


## CHAPTER 14｜The Vision of My Own Death｜我死去的預言

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：857**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Sera sees herself executed by a woman wearing her own face, a vision more terrifying than the war. Sera asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。瑟拉看見自己遭一名長得與她相同的女人處決，這比戰爭更可怕。瑟拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Tarin arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Sera could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 塔林帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑟拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The admiral had inquired about the change and received an instruction bearing the chancellor's seal. A traveler led investigators astray by repeating a rumor, then corrected himself when shown the actual path. The dockworkers' brotherhood promised to protect the young courier without deciding his testimony for him. A witness with diabetes asked for a break before the lengthy examination continued. The court needed to differentiate a signature proving receipt from one authorizing a change of wording. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 上將曾詢問為何改道，得到一份蓋有宰相印的指令。 一名旅人重述流言而使調查員走錯路，看到真實路線後便更正。 碼頭工人兄弟會承諾保護年輕信差，不替他決定證詞。 一名患糖尿病的證人要求在冗長盤問繼續前休息。 法庭必須區分證明收件的簽名與授權改字的簽名。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. A forgotten portrait suggests the figure is a past oracle, not a supernatural copy of Sera. For a long moment no one spoke. Sera understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Tarin broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。一幅遭遺忘的肖像顯示，畫中人是上一代預言官，不是瑟拉的超自然分身。一時之間無人開口。瑟拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。塔林打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The fishermen feared that a closed harbor would doom their winter livelihoods. Excess water collected in the underpass, so the search was postponed until engineers approved entry. The judges considered it feasible to secure the documents without exposing workers to the flooded passage. A feminine style of handwriting was noted in one margin, but Maelin warned against guessing the writer's identity. It was imperative to photograph the packing slips before moving them from the damp floor. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 漁民擔心港口關閉將毀掉冬季生計。 通道積水過多，搜索因此延後，直到工程師准許進入。 法官認為可以在不讓工人冒險進入淹水通道的情況下保全文件。 一處頁邊字跡看似女性筆法，瑟拉卻警告不可據此猜作者身分。 在把包裝單移出潮濕地面前，先拍照是絕對必要的。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Sera insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Tarin drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑟拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。塔林畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The court forbade guards to inflict punishment on the junior officer before charges were established. Maelin sent a formal letter to inquire about the missing procurement authorization. A volunteer offered psychotherapy services to witnesses distressed by repeated threats. Keir measured the radius of the old harbor turning circle to check whether the delivery wagon could pass. The chancellor's reign over the records office had lasted longer than any clerk could remember. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 法庭禁止衛兵在指控成立前懲罰年輕軍官。 瑟拉正式發函詢問遺失的採購授權。 一名志工為因反覆威脅而受創的證人提供心理治療服務。 凱爾測量舊港迴轉圈半徑，確認運貨車能否通過。 宰相掌管紀錄辦公室的時期長到書記們都記不得起點。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The investigation exposed a shortcoming in the rule that allowed one official to approve every copy. Nessa told the apprentice to tuck each fragile receipt into a numbered protective sleeve. The underpass near the canal would close whenever the flood warning reached the red level. A dim underpass connected the market warehouse to the old wharf beneath the city wall. Iva described the woe caused by a border rule that treated her neighbors' homes as cargo. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 調查揭露一項制度缺點：單一官員竟可核准所有副本。 妮莎叫學徒把每張脆弱收據塞入有編號的保護套。 運河旁地下通道在洪水警報達紅色時必須封閉。 昏暗地下通道把市場倉庫連到城牆下的舊碼頭。 伊娃描述邊界規定把鄰居家園當貨物所造成的悲苦。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Sera passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Tarin paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑟拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。塔林停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Sera sees herself executed by a woman wearing her own face, a vision more terrifying than the war.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瑟拉看見自己遭一名長得與她相同的女人處決，這比戰爭更可怕。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. A forgotten portrait suggests the figure is a past oracle, not a supernatural copy of Sera.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 一幅遭遺忘的肖像顯示，畫中人是上一代預言官，不是瑟拉的超自然分身。


## CHAPTER 15｜The Previous Oracle｜上一任預言官

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：834**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. The retired oracle admits she once forged a warning after the court threatened her family. Sera checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。退休預言官承認曾因家人遭威脅而偽造警告。瑟拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Sera asked Tarin to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。瑟拉請塔林說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The royal translation guild was an affiliate of the academy, not an independent authority to change treaties. Nessa hoped to aspire to a public service in which ordinary readers could challenge errors. The delegates stood on the brink of voting before the missing linguistic approval was revealed. In the courtyard, a sparrow hopped beside a ceramic bowl of water. Crowds began to chant for an immediate verdict, but the council chair insisted on completing review. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 王室翻譯公會是學院的附屬組織，無權獨立更改條約。 妮莎希望追求一種讓普通讀者也能質疑錯誤的公共服務。 代表正要表決時，缺失的語文核准程序才被揭露。 」瑟拉擔心群眾的興奮會分散對證據的注意。 群眾開始高喊要求立即判決，議長卻堅持完成審查。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. She offers evidence against the general, but asks that her own wrongdoing remain on record. Sera felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。她願作證指控元帥，也要求記錄自己的過失。瑟拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: An old chimpanzee sketch in the academy's teaching collection showed that the library stored materials beyond law. The school offered a meal coupon to students who had traveled far to watch the public session. Maelin would not let a distraction in the gallery replace the missing signatures as the central issue. The examiner checked that every delegation had received the same text before the proposed vote. Maelin's instinctive desire to defend herself weakened when she saw how many families remained unheard. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 學院教學藏品中一幅老黑猩猩素描，顯示圖書館收藏不只限法律。 學校提供餐券給遠道來觀察公開會議的學生。 瑟拉不讓旁聽席的干擾取代缺失簽名這個核心問題。 審查員確認表決前每個代表團都收到相同文本。 瑟拉本能地想替自己辯護，但看見仍未被聽見的家庭後，這股衝動減弱。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Marshal Dorian demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Sera replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當多里安元帥要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑟拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Residents feared that winter crops would perish if the grain remained locked away. No delegate could ratify the revised wording before the full three-language review was completed. A reckless proposal to arrest every palace clerk was rejected for lacking individual evidence. The printer offered to reproduce the original versions side by side once the court permitted publication. A sergeant maintained order without preventing peaceful questions from the assembly. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 居民擔心若糧食繼續被封存，冬季作物會枯死。 完整三語審查完成前，任何代表都不能批准修改措辭。 一項魯莽提案要求逮捕所有宮廷書記，因缺乏個別證據而遭否決。 印刷工願在法庭允許後把原始版本並排複製。 一名中士維持秩序，但不阻止議會提出和平質詢。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A sparrow landed on the hearing room's open window while the vote was postponed. A public subscription fund helped pay for independent copies of the disputed documents. The council postponed ratification, and thereafter every delegate could propose a correction in writing. A vocational school offered to train new clerks in document custody and translation checks. After the exhausting debate, Maelin tried a brief yoga exercise to settle her breathing before testimony. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 表決延期時，一隻麻雀停在聽證室開著的窗上。 公開認捐基金協助支付爭議文件的獨立副本費用。 議會延後追認，此後每位代表都可書面提出更正。 一間職業學校願培訓新書記學習文件保管與翻譯核對。 漫長辯論後，瑟拉做了簡短瑜伽呼吸練習，才準備作證。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Sera resisted the urge to call it proof. Tarin opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑟拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。塔林翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**

   - B. The retired oracle admits she once forged a warning after the court threatened her family.

## CHAPTER 16｜The Emperor's Favorite Disaster｜皇帝最愛的災難

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：851**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Dorian presents a vision of the emperor's assassination to justify martial law throughout the capital. Sera knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。多里安拿皇帝遭刺殺的預言，要求首都全面戒嚴。瑟拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Sera could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Tarin suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，瑟拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。塔林提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The guild offered a calligraphy demonstration to show how easily decorative signatures could be imitated. A porter helped move heavy medical boxes to the shelter, their cartons packed beneath cardboard covers. Nessa numbered each carton before moving the recovered materials into court storage. The public kitchen agreed to cater the long demonstration without accepting money from the accused official. A medical notice about cholesterol levels had accidentally been packed with the treaty printing receipts. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 公會安排書法示範，說明裝飾性簽名有多容易被模仿。 一名搬運工協助把沉重醫療箱送到避難所，紙箱外包著硬紙板。 妮莎在把尋回材料移入法庭倉庫前，替每個紙箱編號。 公共廚房同意為漫長示範提供餐食，卻不接受被控官員的資助。 一張關於膽固醇數值的醫療通知意外和條約印刷收據裝在一起。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. Sera identifies a repairable failure in the palace ventilation system that would cause the very panic he predicts. Tarin asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Sera agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。瑟拉找到宮廷通風設備的可修復故障，正是預言中恐慌的來源。塔林詢問是否可能只是出錯，瑟拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: An unexpected disturbance in the courtyard did not stop the independent examiners from measuring the plate. The paper fibers pointed downward in the old impression but across the new one. Workers were indignant that their wages had been diverted to an unauthorized political printing job. The apprentice called the plate-making technique a marvel, while Nessa reminded him that skill could be misused. A neutral facilitator was asked to mediate the dispute over access to the workshop tools. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 庭院突然傳來騷動，仍未阻止獨立檢驗員測量印版。 舊壓痕的紙纖維向下，新壓痕卻橫向延伸。 工人對工資被挪作未授權政治印刷工作感到憤怒。 學徒讚嘆製版技術是奇蹟，妮莎提醒他技術也可能被濫用。 各方請一名中立協調者調解使用工坊工具的爭議。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Sera had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Tarin proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑟拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。塔林提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The witnesses rejected the claim that the altered wording was a miraculous accident of ink and paper. Keir used a wooden paddle to steer the evidence boat away from the flooded dock. A persistent clerk located the separate payment account after searching months of stationery receipts. The station porter carried the sealed boxes only after signing the custody log. The island workshops used sand filters to purify water during the late summer shortage. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 證人反對宣稱改動措辭是墨水與紙張造成的神奇意外。 凱爾以木槳將載證物的小船划離淹水碼頭。 一名堅持不懈的書記翻找數月文具收據後，找到獨立付款帳戶。 車站搬運工簽署保管紀錄後才搬動封箱。 島上工坊在夏末缺水時以砂濾器淨化飲水。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Nessa asked volunteers to rehearse the evidence transfer before the public examination began. A messenger would relay the judge's decision to all three delegations without paraphrasing it. The false certificate was typed on palace stationery that had been withdrawn the previous year. A superstitious servant feared the altered seal was cursed, but Maelin asked him for facts he had witnessed. An old walnut tree shaded the courtyard where the witnesses waited for separate interviews. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 公開檢驗開始前，妮莎請志工預演證物交接。 信差會把法官決定原文轉告三方代表，不自行改述。 假證明使用去年已停用的王宮信紙。 一名迷信僕人害怕被改印章受詛咒，瑟拉請他只說親眼所見。 一棵老胡桃樹替等待分別訪談的證人遮蔭。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Sera returned to a cracked prophecy lens. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Tarin remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the imperial observatory waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑟拉再次看向一枚裂開的預言透鏡。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。塔林守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的帝國觀星臺等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. Dorian presents a vision of the emperor's assassination to justify martial law throughout the capital.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 多里安拿皇帝遭刺殺的預言，要求首都全面戒嚴。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Sera identifies a repairable failure in the palace ventilation system that would cause the very panic he predicts.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瑟拉找到宮廷通風設備的可修復故障，正是預言中恐慌的來源。


## CHAPTER 17｜The Prohibition on Certainty｜禁止宣稱確定

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：852**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. An independent assembly forbids officials from describing any future as inevitable without specifying its conditions. Sera had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the imperial observatory, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。獨立大會禁止官員在沒有說明條件時，把任何未來描述為不可避免。瑟拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在帝國觀星臺，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Sera asked Tarin not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑟拉請塔林不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An analytical report separated proven payments from assumptions about who had written the forbidden clause. Chancellor Varr answered the sabotage report with a campaign to boycott the academy and its suppliers. The storm drill recorded one minor casualty, and the committee ordered a safer route. Anika offered consolation to families whose compensation claims were still waiting for review. Nessa secured copies of correspondence between the chancellor's office and the secret printer. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 分析報告把已證實的付款與對誰寫下違規條款的猜測分開。 瓦爾宰相用抵制學院及其供應商的運動回應破壞報告。 風暴演練記錄了一名輕傷者，委員會命令改用更安全路線。 安妮卡安慰補償申請仍待審閱的家庭。 妮莎取得宰相辦公室與祕密印刷商往來信件副本。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Sera accepts limits on her authority, even though the restrictions weaken her position at court. Sera watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Tarin recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。瑟拉接受自身權限限制，即使這讓她在宮廷的地位下降。瑟拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。塔林公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The schools planned to cultivate skill in all three treaty languages rather than teach one as superior. The financial disclosure named the officials who had authorized the unusual payments. Maelin feared that a single hidden error might survive for eternity if later generations copied it blindly. A trainee joked that he would flunk the new examination if it demanded perfect memory instead of careful checking. The shelter insisted on basic hygiene standards even during the transport workers' strike. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 學校計畫培養三種條約語言的能力，而非把某一種當作較高等。 財務揭露文件列出核准異常付款的官員姓名。 瑟拉擔心若後代盲目照抄，一項隱藏錯誤可能永遠存在。 實習生開玩笑說，新考試若要求完美記憶而非仔細核對，他肯定不及格。 即使運輸工人罷工，避難所仍堅持基本衛生標準。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Marshal Dorian and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Sera rejected both. The issue was whether fear of disaster justifies the empire’s wars, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住多里安元帥的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑟拉兩者都拒絕。核心是對災難的恐懼是否足以合理化帝國發動戰爭，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The court refused to kindle hostility by publishing an unverified list of suspected clerks. The printer's workshop layout showed a back door near the route recorded in the carrier's ledger. Unauthorized printing proved lucrative for a contractor paid from two separate budgets. The outward sign of the guild looked respectable, although its purchasing records required close examination. A preventive order protected the archive doors against removal of materials without a signed inventory. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 法庭拒絕公布未經核實的嫌疑書記名單，以免煽動敵意。 印刷坊平面配置圖顯示後門靠近信差帳冊所載路線。 未授權印刷讓同時從兩筆預算領款的承包商獲利可觀。 公會外部招牌看來體面，但採購紀錄仍需嚴格審查。 預防性命令禁止任何人未附簽署清單就移出檔案室物品。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A child let out a shriek when the sudden alarm interrupted the quiet witness room. The old courier began to sneeze from dust, and the court permitted a short break. A school proposed reading games as stimulation for children learning the three official scripts. The repeated paperwork felt tiresome, yet every receipt made false claims harder to sustain. Two workers had to wrestle a heavy storage door open after a broken hinge jammed it. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 突如其來的警報打斷安靜證人室時，一名孩子尖叫。 老信差因灰塵開始打噴嚏，法庭准許短暫休息。 學校提出閱讀遊戲，作為學習三種正式文字孩子的刺激活動。 反覆文書工作令人厭煩，但每張收據都讓假說法更難維持。 鉸鏈卡住後，兩名工人得費力扳開沉重的儲物門。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Sera had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Tarin wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Marshal Dorian. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑟拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。塔林把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起多里安元帥注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. An independent assembly forbids officials from describing any future as inevitable without specifying its conditions.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 獨立大會禁止官員在沒有說明條件時，把任何未來描述為不可避免。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Sera accepts limits on her authority, even though the restrictions weaken her position at court.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 瑟拉接受自身權限限制，即使這讓她在宮廷的地位下降。


## CHAPTER 18｜The Bridge Rebuilt｜重建的橋

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：851**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Engineers repair the original victory bridge while soldiers and refugees work side by side. Sera saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。工程師修復最初的凱旋橋，士兵與難民並肩合作。瑟拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Sera listened longer than the schedule allowed. Tarin reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑟拉聽得比議程允許的更久。塔林提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The delegates waited awhile before the coastal weather office confirmed that the storm warning was genuine. The emergency council agreed to broaden access to shelters beyond the families named in palace registers. A lighthouse on the cape sent warnings to boats approaching the unsafe southern channel. No guard could hold a traveler captive merely because the treaty's wording was under review. Nessa noticed that one missing consonant changed the meaning of the shortened river phrase. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 代表等了一會兒，沿海氣象站才確認暴風警報屬實。 緊急議會同意擴大避難所使用資格，不只限王宮名冊上的家庭。 岬角上的燈塔向接近危險南航道的船隻發出警報。 衛兵不能只因條約措辭正在審查，就把旅客拘禁。 妮莎注意到遺失一個子音便改變了河語縮寫句的意思。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Sera sees the old disaster again, but this time the altered supports change the result. Sera read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Tarin did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。瑟拉再次看見舊災難，這次改良過的支柱改變了結局。瑟拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。塔林沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The palace could not dictate an unlimited closure when the signed originals required review dates. A dwarf apple tree grew beside the shelter door, its branches bent by the rising wind. An excerpt of the genuine safety clause was posted at every inspected crossing. A suspected intruder at the record room turned out to be a lost volunteer seeking blankets. The mischievous child admitted he had rung the wrong bell during the storm drill. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 簽署原件要求複查日期，王宮便不能命令無期限封閉。 避難所門邊長著一棵矮小蘋果樹，枝條被增強的風吹彎。 每個通過檢查的渡口都張貼真實安全條款節錄。 紀錄室一名疑似闖入者，結果只是迷路尋找毛毯的志工。 一個調皮孩子承認自己在風暴演練時按錯鐘。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Sera insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Tarin drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑟拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。塔林畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The rescue operative confirmed that the replacement road remained clear for medical wagons. The rescue mission became an ordeal when volunteers had to move families through heavy rain. An outlaw gang tried to charge unofficial passage fees but fled when the district officers arrived. A pinch of salt was added to the emergency bread, and the kitchen kept a record of supplies. Every closure notice had to precede the order to move travelers onto the alternative route. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 救援人員確認替代道路仍可供醫療馬車通行。 志工必須冒著大雨疏散家庭時，救援任務成了一場嚴峻考驗。 一群不法幫派企圖收取非法通行費，區域官員到場後便逃走。 緊急麵包加入一撮鹽，廚房保留物資紀錄。 每份封路公告必須先於將旅客改道的命令發布。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The restoration of the damaged bridge required inspection by engineers from both riverbanks. The statute limited emergency restrictions to seven days unless independent review renewed them. A tornado remained possible farther south, but the local warning named only the verified risks. Wholesale food traders agreed to send supplies without demanding that residents sign the disputed land forms. Keir used a magnifying lens to zoom in on the faded serial number of a harbor gate. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 受損橋梁的修復須經兩岸工程師共同檢查。 法規限制緊急禁令最多七天，除非獨立審查續期。 更南方可能出現龍捲風，當地警報卻只列已證實的風險。 批發食品商同意供貨，不要求居民簽署有爭議的土地文件。 凱爾用放大鏡放大查看港門褪色的序號。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Sera passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Tarin paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑟拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。塔林停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. Engineers repair the original victory bridge while soldiers and refugees work side by side.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 工程師修復最初的凱旋橋，士兵與難民並肩合作。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. Sera sees the old disaster again, but this time the altered supports change the result.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 瑟拉再次看見舊災難，這次改良過的支柱改變了結局。


## CHAPTER 19｜The Hidden War Budget｜隱藏的戰爭預算

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：861**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Sera refused to begin with the official conclusion. Tarin uncovers payments made to people who staged accidents used as evidence for emergency invasions. In the imperial observatory, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。瑟拉拒絕從官方結論開始。塔林查出有人收錢製造事故，再把事故用作緊急侵略的證據。在帝國觀星臺，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Tarin arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Sera could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 塔林帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑟拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Local workers tried to fortify the temporary shelter before the next wave of rain. Vask arrived in glamorous robes, hoping ceremony would distract the court from the payment records. Maelin would not infer ownership from a shipment label that mentioned only temporary custody. The clerk's intonation changed when he repeated the phrase the chancellor had dictated. The old museum labels invariably credited the crown, regardless of who had made the objects. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 地方工人試圖在下一波雨前加固臨時避難所。 瓦斯克穿著華麗長袍到場，希望儀式轉移法庭對付款紀錄的注意。 瑟拉不願僅憑寫著臨時保管的運輸標籤推論所有權。 書記重述宰相口述的句子時，語調發生改變。 舊館藏標籤不論製作者是誰，總把功勞歸給王室。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The records implicate Dorian’s office but do not prove every order came from him personally. For a long moment no one spoke. Sera understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Tarin broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。紀錄指向多里安辦公室，卻未證明每道命令皆由他本人下達。一時之間無人開口。瑟拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。塔林打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The courier had stopped at an isle station where a second route ledger was kept. Maelin tried to meditate before giving evidence, but the lost harvests kept returning to her thoughts. A migrant worker identified the warehouse where the altered pamphlets had been stored. The court reminded the crowd that every witness was mortal and could make mistakes. A nude figure in an old guild painting was covered for the school visitors without altering the artwork itself. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 信差曾停靠一座小島驛站，那裡保存第二份路線帳冊。 瑟拉試著在作證前冥想，失去的收成卻不斷浮現腦海。 一名移工指認存放修改版小冊子的倉庫。 法庭提醒群眾，每名證人都是凡人，都可能犯錯。 為了參訪的學生，公會舊畫中的裸體人物被暫時遮住，但畫作未被改動。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Marshal Dorian demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Sera replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當多里安元帥要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑟拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The courier took an oath to describe what he carried, not what he imagined was inside the box. A strong odor from the workshop solvent forced the hearing to move into an open courtyard. The crowd called the secret payment outrageous, but the judge still demanded exact receipts. A weather clerk recorded a high ozone reading while the storm passed over the distant hills. The courier kept a packet of route permits that matched the delivery book dates. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 信差宣誓只描述運送過什麼，不猜測箱內物品。 工坊溶劑的強烈氣味迫使聽證移到露天庭院。 群眾認為祕密付款令人髮指，法官仍要求準確收據。 風暴越過遠山時，氣象書記記錄到較高的臭氧讀數。 信差保存一包通行許可，日期與送貨簿相符。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Maelin's proficiency in all three languages helped her identify the substituted phrase, not its author. A hostile minister showed scorn for the village witnesses until Iva produced the original receipt. The customs captain promised to investigate whether anyone had tried to smuggle replacement plates across the river. The courier took a steady stride toward the witness stand despite the threats he had received. A trace of vanilla oil on the packing cloth linked two shipments from the same supplier. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 瑟拉精通三種語言，使她能辨認被換句子，但不能據此確認作者。 一名敵意大臣輕蔑村民證人，直到伊娃拿出原始收據。 海關隊長承諾調查是否有人企圖走私替換印版過河。 儘管受威脅，信差仍穩步走向證人席。 包裝布上的一絲香草油，把兩批貨物連到同一供應商。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Sera resisted the urge to call it proof. Tarin opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑟拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。塔林翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. Tarin uncovers payments made to people who staged accidents used as evidence for emergency invasions.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 塔林查出有人收錢製造事故，再把事故用作緊急侵略的證據。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The records implicate Dorian’s office but do not prove every order came from him personally.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 紀錄指向多里安辦公室，卻未證明每道命令皆由他本人下達。


## CHAPTER 20｜The Emperor Must Hear No｜皇帝必須聽見不

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：857**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Sera tells the emperor that a ruler who sees only disasters has no right to use fear as a substitute for consent. Sera asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。瑟拉告訴皇帝，只看見災難的統治者沒有權利以恐懼取代人民同意。瑟拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Sera asked Tarin to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。瑟拉請塔林說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: In accordance with the signed treaty, each community could request an independent review of a boundary change. A witness recalled that during adolescence she had learned both river and hill scripts at school. A caterpillar crawled across the weathered survey pole while Keir measured the original mark. The old compass wheel turned clockwise, confirming which direction the survey team had recorded. A brass compass from the fishing exhibit was placed in protective storage before the storm. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 依照簽署條約，每個社區都可以要求獨立審查邊界變動。 一名證人回憶自己青春期在學校學過河語與山語文字。 凱爾測量原始刻痕時，一條毛毛蟲爬過風化測量桿。 舊羅盤輪順時針轉動，確認測量團隊記錄的方向。 風暴前，一只漁業展的黃銅羅盤被移入保護庫。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. The emperor refuses her advice until the public assembly threatens to withhold the war levy. Sera felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。皇帝拒絕建議，直到公開大會威脅停止繳納戰爭稅。瑟拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The public map appeared to contradict the originals by placing the village on crown land. The judge rejected a dubious account that could not be tied to any dated record. A small enhancement to the map key made the temporary crossings visible to ordinary readers. Chancellor Varr's lawyer raised a dubious objection, arguing that a monarch could exempt his own office from scrutiny. Cattle continued to graze on the meadow while its ownership remained under review. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 公開地圖把村莊劃入王室土地，看似與原件矛盾。 法官拒絕採用無法與附日期紀錄連結的可疑說法。 地圖圖例的小幅改良讓普通讀者也看得出臨時渡口。 瓦爾律師提出可疑的異議，主張君主可以豁免自己的辦公室接受審查。 所有權仍在審查期間，牛群繼續在草地上吃草。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Sera had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Tarin proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑟拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。塔林提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Maelin had a hunch about the altered route, but she still asked for Keir's measurements. The surveyor's intellect did not free him from the duty to publish his calculations. Vask tried to turn the dispute into a matter of nationalism rather than the wording of shared rights. An ornament on the treaty chest concealed a small mark used by the original custodians. The river guard offered a salute to the visiting Hill Council delegates before the joint inspection. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 瑟拉對遭改路線有直覺猜想，仍要求凱爾提供測量數據。 測量員再聰明，也不能免於公布計算過程的責任。 瓦斯克試圖把爭端變成民族主義問題，而非共同權利的措辭問題。 條約箱上的裝飾藏著原始保管人使用的小記號。 共同檢查前，河岸衛兵向來訪山地議會代表敬禮。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Nessa warned that a sloppy comparison could damage the case more than an honest admission of uncertainty. A solitary survey marker stood beyond the meadow, untouched by the new road construction. Every staple in the copied map packet was photographed before the sheets were separated. The commission agreed to summon the officer responsible for the disputed survey approval. A child had to tiptoe around the tables where the original maps lay drying. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 妮莎警告，草率比對比誠實承認不確定更可能傷害案件。 一根孤立界標立在草地外，未受新道路工程碰觸。 地圖副本包裡每枚釘書針都在拆頁前拍照記錄。 委員會同意傳喚負責爭議測量核准的官員。 原始地圖攤在桌上晾乾，孩子只能踮腳繞過桌子。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Sera returned to a cracked prophecy lens. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Tarin remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the imperial observatory waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑟拉再次看向一枚裂開的預言透鏡。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。塔林守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的帝國觀星臺等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。
