### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Sera tells the emperor that a ruler who sees only disasters has no right to use fear as a substitute for consent.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瑟拉告訴皇帝，只看見災難的統治者沒有權利以恐懼取代人民同意。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The emperor refuses her advice until the public assembly threatens to withhold the war levy.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 皇帝拒絕建議，直到公開大會威脅停止繳納戰爭稅。


## CHAPTER 21｜A Future Not Ordered｜沒有奉命的未來

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：868**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. On the eve of the invasion, citizens use the public map to move supplies and prevent a predicted famine. Sera checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。侵略前夕，公民使用公開地圖調動物資，阻止預言中的飢荒。瑟拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Sera could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Tarin suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，瑟拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。塔林提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A school exhibition about aviation included an old route chart that helped explain how distant notices were delivered. Beforehand, investigators had tested every copy of the island packet against original seals and accounting registers. Nessa prepared a booklet displaying the full original and the altered public summary side by side. A scholar examined the cellular structure of a pressed leaf found between two treaty pages. The duration of each emergency road closure had to appear on the notice itself. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 學校航空展有一張舊路線圖，幫助說明遠方公告如何送達。 調查員事前已將島上文件包的每份副本與原始印璽、帳簿逐一核對。 妮莎準備一本小冊子，把完整原件與遭改公開摘要並排呈現。 一名學者檢查夾在兩頁條約之間的壓乾樹葉細胞結構。 每份緊急封路公告必須標明持續時間。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. The army’s own families demand a pause, leaving Dorian without the support needed to advance. Tarin asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Sera agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。軍人家屬要求停戰，多里安失去進軍需要的支持。塔林詢問是否可能只是出錯，瑟拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Local folklore remembered the three river stones as signs of cooperation, not conquest. Witnesses reported repeated harassment after asking the palace to release the original treaty copies. An imperial order could not lawfully replace a treaty already signed by three independent assemblies. The imposing palace hall looked less frightening when community representatives were allowed to ask questions. The tribunal could oblige the archive to disclose its register but could not force a witness to guess. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 地方民間傳說把三塊河石視為合作而非征服的象徵。 證人說要求王宮公開原始條約後，便遭到反覆騷擾。 帝國命令不能合法取代三個獨立議會已簽署的條約。 社區代表獲准提問後，宏偉的宮廷大廳不再那麼嚇人。 法庭可以要求檔案室公開登記冊，卻不能逼證人猜測。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Marshal Dorian and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Sera rejected both. The issue was whether fear of disaster justifies the empire’s wars, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住多里安元帥的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑟拉兩者都拒絕。核心是對災難的恐懼是否足以合理化帝國發動戰爭，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Vask seemed to obsess over the appearance of the seals rather than the actual changes in the words. An octopus painted on an old trading flag marked the shipping firm that carried the sealed plate case. Workers described how the ministry had used threats to oppress those who reported failures. The meeting organizer ensured that translators from all three territories could speak for themselves. Their report also identified a peril in continuing to operate the network without new safety rules. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 瓦斯克似乎過度執著於印章外觀，而非文字真正改動。 舊貿易旗上的章魚圖案標示了運送封存印版箱的船公司。 工人描述部門如何威脅回報故障的人，壓迫他們保持沉默。 會議組織者確保三地譯員都能自己發言。 報告也指出，若不訂新安全規則就繼續使用網路，將有重大危險。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A poultry farmer told the court that his family had lost livestock when their barn froze overnight. A prehistoric tool displayed at the museum had no role in the case, despite a witness's excited claim. Iva quoted an old proverb about promises being measured by what people actually do. A children's drawing of a spacecraft reminded Maelin that their futures should extend beyond border fear. A deep wrinkle appeared on the judge's forehead as the earlier printing date was read aloud. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一名養禽農民告訴法庭，家裡的牲畜因穀倉整夜結凍而死。 博物館展示的一件史前工具與本案無關，儘管證人激動地聲稱有關。 伊娃引用古老諺語：承諾要看人實際做了什麼。 孩子畫的太空船提醒瑟拉，他們的未來不該被邊界恐懼限制。 更早的印製日期被朗讀時，法官額頭上出現深深皺紋。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Sera had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Tarin wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Marshal Dorian. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑟拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。塔林把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起多里安元帥注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. On the eve of the invasion, citizens use the public map to move supplies and prevent a predicted famine.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 侵略前夕，公民使用公開地圖調動物資，阻止預言中的飢荒。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The army’s own families demand a pause, leaving Dorian without the support needed to advance.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 軍人家屬要求停戰，多里安失去進軍需要的支持。


## CHAPTER 22｜The Judgment of Probability｜機率的判決

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：871**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. An inquiry separates genuine failed forecasts from deliberately staged disasters and forged state reports. Sera knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。調查把真實失敗預報、蓄意製造的事故與偽造政府報告分別處理。瑟拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Sera asked Tarin not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑟拉請塔林不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The three delegations stood for the shared anthem before the financial hearing began. Keir had to ascend the narrow stairs to retrieve an old road invoice from the upper archive. The treasury audit separated the cost of illegal printing from legitimate emergency communication. Vask called the river witnesses barbarian, and the judge ordered the insult struck from the official record. A defect in the accounting system allowed one official to approve the same payment under two names. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 財務聽證開始前，三方代表起立聆聽共同頌歌。 凱爾得爬上狹窄樓梯，到檔案室上層取回舊道路發票。 國庫稽核把非法印製費用與合法緊急通信開支分開。 瓦斯克稱河民證人是野蠻人，法官命令從正式紀錄刪除侮辱語。 帳務系統的缺陷讓一名官員能用兩個名稱核准同一付款。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Dorian faces charges for the proven financial scheme, while the court declines to criminalize the act of predicting. Sera watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Tarin recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。多里安因已證實的資金陰謀遭起訴，法院拒絕把預言行為本身當罪。瑟拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。塔林公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Unpaid bridge workers began to grumble when the treasury explained where their wages had gone. Heavy hail damaged the archive roof, so certified copies were moved to a drier public building. The river clinic offered healthful meals to elderly witnesses waiting through the long proceedings. A tall hedge outside the tribunal had concealed the back entrance used by the secret carrier. The pharmacist testified that no illegal heroin shipment had passed the route, despite rumors used to discredit the witnesses. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 國庫解釋工資去向時，未獲薪的橋工開始抱怨。 猛烈冰雹損壞檔案室屋頂，認證副本因此移往較乾燥的公共建物。 河岸診所為等待冗長程序的年長證人提供健康餐食。 法庭外高高的樹籬曾遮住祕密信差使用的後門。 藥師作證說並無非法海洛因貨物經過該路線，反駁用來抹黑證人的流言。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Sera insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Tarin drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑟拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。塔林畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The court respected intimacy and barred questions about a witness's private relationship unless legally relevant. The mainland workshop had been paid from the same account that funded the suspicious shipping route. Officials and villagers could mingle in the courtyard but were told not to discuss protected testimony. The pharmacist confirmed that heroin was not part of the clinic's authorized medicine inventory. A polar weather map was displayed to explain why the winter roads had required early repairs. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 法庭尊重親密隱私，禁止詢問與案件無關的證人私人關係。 大陸上的工坊從資助可疑運輸路線的同一帳戶收款。 官員與村民可在庭院交談，卻不得討論受保護證詞。 藥師證實海洛因不在診所核准藥品庫存中。 現場展示極地天氣圖，解釋冬季道路為何需要提早修繕。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The old interpreter had chosen to reside near the ferry so he could hear everyday river speech. The surviving storage paper was resistant to damp, which helped experts establish its earlier date. The witness's stepmother verified that the family had never received a payment for surrendering its land. Vask claimed the printing cost a trillion coins, but the receipts showed a far smaller and traceable sum. Nessa kept the sealed chest upright while moving the documents away from the leaking roof. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 老譯員選擇住在渡口附近，才能聽到日常河語。 倖存的儲藏紙具有防潮性，有助專家確認較早日期。 證人的繼母確認家人從未收到交出土地的補償款。 瓦斯克誇稱印製花了一兆枚硬幣，但收據顯示金額小得多且可追查。 妮莎把封存箱直立搬離漏水屋頂，保護其中文件。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Sera passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Tarin paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑟拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。塔林停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. An inquiry separates genuine failed forecasts from deliberately staged disasters and forged state reports.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 調查把真實失敗預報、蓄意製造的事故與偽造政府報告分別處理。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Dorian faces charges for the proven financial scheme, while the court declines to criminalize the act of predicting.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 多里安因已證實的資金陰謀遭起訴，法院拒絕把預言行為本身當罪。


## CHAPTER 23｜The Archive of Alternatives｜替代未來的檔案

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：859**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Sera helps open a library containing all the futures that might be prevented, with uncertainty displayed beside each projection. Sera had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the imperial observatory, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。瑟拉協助開放圖書館，收藏所有可能避免的未來，並在每份預測旁標示不確定性。瑟拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在帝國觀星臺，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Sera listened longer than the schedule allowed. Tarin reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑟拉聽得比議程允許的更久。塔林提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The bridge team worked to avert another delay in delivering medicine to the isolated villages. The witness house caretaker provided an accurate list of the nights the courier had stayed there. Beneath a collapsed ceiling, inspectors found a cavity in the wall containing old ration tickets and labor lists. The court asked Nessa to condense the technical explanation without removing the disputed phrase. Maelin felt dismay when she saw how long the displaced families had waited for compensation. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 橋梁團隊努力避免向孤立村莊送藥再次延誤。 證人屋管理人提供信差曾住宿各夜的準確名單。 倒塌屋頂下，檢查員發現牆裡有空腔，藏著舊配給券與工人名單。 法庭請妮莎濃縮技術說明，但不得刪掉爭議詞句。 瑟拉看到流離失所家庭等補償已久，感到沮喪。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Ordinary people learn to question visions rather than worship or fear them. Sera read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Tarin did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。普通人學會質疑預言，而不是膜拜或恐懼。瑟拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。塔林沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: An emigration ledger proved that some families had left the border only after the false tax began. The tribunal found a serious flaw in the process that permitted one signature to override three texts. Iva asked for an inclusive compensation hearing that admitted speakers of every treaty language. A palace agent attempted to intimidate the apprentice, but the judge granted him legal counsel. The workshop owner might be liable for destroying records even if he had not authored the altered words. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 移民帳冊證明一些家庭在不實稅費開始後才離開邊界。 法庭發現程序有重大缺陷：一個簽名竟能凌駕三份文本。 伊娃要求包容性的補償聽證，讓所有條約語言使用者都能參與。 一名王宮代理人企圖恐嚇學徒，法官准許他得到法律顧問。 即使工坊主人未撰寫修改字句，若銷毀紀錄仍可能負法律責任。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Marshal Dorian demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Sera replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當多里安元帥要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑟拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A charity lottery raised money for the bridge repairs without touching the disputed grain fund. The nearsighted clerk requested larger print so he could read his statement accurately before signing. A frightened apprentice had a pimple on his chin and kept touching it while waiting to testify. The revelation of the hidden rubbing connected the written order to the actual metal plate. Nessa would not shred an obsolete draft because even rejected versions could explain the sequence of changes. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 慈善彩券為修橋募款，沒有動用有爭議的糧食基金。 近視書記要求較大字體，以便簽名前準確閱讀證詞。 一名緊張學徒下巴有顆青春痘，等作證時不斷摸它。 祕密拓印的揭露，把書面命令與實際金屬印版連接起來。 妮莎不肯粉碎過期草稿，因為被否決版本也能解釋修改順序。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The apprentice made a solemn promise to describe only what he had witnessed in the workshop. The packed hearing room felt so hot that several witnesses feared they might suffocate. A surge of public anger followed the evidence, but the guards kept an open route for witnesses to leave. The judges looked upward when a loose ceiling panel rattled during the storm. The workforce from the print shop demanded payment for lawful shifts and protection against collective blame. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 學徒鄭重承諾，只描述自己在工坊親眼看見的事。 擁擠的聽證室熱得令人難受，幾名證人擔心會窒息。 證據提出後民憤激增，衛兵仍保持通道讓證人自由離去。 風暴中天花板鬆板震動，法官抬頭查看。 印刷坊工人要求合法班次的工資，並免於集體受責。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Sera resisted the urge to call it proof. Tarin opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑟拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。塔林翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Sera helps open a library containing all the futures that might be prevented, with uncertainty displayed beside each projection.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瑟拉協助開放圖書館，收藏所有可能避免的未來，並在每份預測旁標示不確定性。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Ordinary people learn to question visions rather than worship or fear them.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 普通人學會質疑預言，而不是膜拜或恐懼。


## CHAPTER 24｜A Day Without a Vision｜沒有預言的一天

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：875**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Sera chooses not to look ahead during the city’s first peace festival, trusting the people who prepared it. Sera saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。首屆和平節當天，瑟拉選擇不看未來，相信親手籌備的民眾。瑟拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** Tarin arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Sera could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 塔林帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑟拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The court would proceed accordingly once the three assemblies approved the corrected wording. An alarm device began to beep when a clerk opened the evidence cabinet without entering the access code. The chairperson asked each language delegation to confirm that it had received the complete final text. Maelin rejected the idea that one kingdom had to civilize the others before their words deserved respect. A fisher brought a clam shell marked with the old village route as a reminder of local history. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 三方議會核准更正文字後，法庭將依此繼續程序。 一名書記未輸入密碼就打開證物櫃，警報裝置開始嗶嗶作響。 議長請各語言代表團確認已收到完整定稿。 瑟拉反對某國必須先教化別國，其語言才值得尊重的想法。 一名漁夫帶來標著村落舊路線的蛤蜊殼，提醒大家地方歷史。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. For once she remembers the day clearly, with all its uncertain and ordinary joy. For a long moment no one spoke. Sera understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Tarin broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。她終於能清楚記得這一天，包括其中不確定而平凡的喜悅。一時之間無人開口。瑟拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。塔林打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The coalition of river, hill, and royal delegates agreed to preserve every earlier version in the archive. A conscientious registrar checked every correction note against the signed original before publication. The families gathered in a cozy schoolroom to read the new bilingual notices aloud. Hungry travelers began to devour the bread served after the lengthy assembly meeting. The disastrous policy had taken more than grain; it had broken trust across the border. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 河民、山地與王室代表組成的聯盟同意保存檔案中的所有舊版。 一位盡責登記官在出版前逐一依簽署原件核對更正註記。 家人聚集在溫暖舒適的教室，朗讀新的雙語公告。 飢餓旅客在漫長會議後大口吃起分發的麵包。 這項災難性政策奪走的不只是糧食，也破壞了邊界兩側的信任。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Sera had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Tarin proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑟拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。塔林提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A murmur of disbelief moved through the room when the final print order was read aloud. The assemblies agreed to formulate future summaries only after each language team approved the complete meaning. Keir explained why the same latitude did not imply that two villages shared one government. Some called the revision patriotic, but Iva said honest agreements should protect people beyond flags. Nessa tried to reckon the unpaid wages by comparing the work log with the treasury payments. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 最後印製命令被朗讀時，現場傳出難以置信的低語。 三方議會同意往後只有各語文團隊確認完整意思後才能制定摘要。 凱爾解釋，即使緯度相同，也不表示兩座村莊屬於同一政府。 有人稱修訂愛國，伊娃說誠實協議應保障國旗下之外的人。 妮莎比對工時簿與國庫付款，試圖計算欠薪總額。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The prophecy lens showed Sera a collapse in a fraction of a second. She wrote down the conditions before the image faded, because a disaster without its causes could be used to justify almost anything. Tarin made her add the words this may be prevented beneath every prediction.

**繁中** 預言透鏡在剎那間讓瑟拉看見崩塌。她趁畫面消失前寫下條件，因為沒有原因的災難影像幾乎可以拿來合理化任何事。塔林要求她在每份預言下加上「此事可能被避免」。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A river's ripple was visible through the windows, small and constant beneath the grand language of peace. The queen's stature could not replace the village assemblies' legal right to verify the treaty. The public transcript preserved every objection raised during the final three-language reading. The tropic climate described in the old atlas differed sharply from the harsh winter faced by the border settlements. A vine curled around the school fence where new treaty notices were posted for families. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 窗外河面泛起細小而持續的漣漪，在宏大的和平語言之下安靜流動。 女王的地位不能取代村民議會依法核對條約的權利。 公開逐字稿保存最後三語宣讀時提出的所有異議。 舊地圖冊描述的熱帶氣候與邊境聚落面對的嚴冬截然不同。 一條藤蔓繞著貼滿新條約公告的學校圍欄。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Sera returned to a cracked prophecy lens. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Tarin remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the imperial observatory waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑟拉再次看向一枚裂開的預言透鏡。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。塔林守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的帝國觀星臺等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. Sera chooses not to look ahead during the city’s first peace festival, trusting the people who prepared it.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 首屆和平節當天，瑟拉選擇不看未來，相信親手籌備的民眾。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. For once she remembers the day clearly, with all its uncertain and ordinary joy.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 她終於能清楚記得這一天，包括其中不確定而平凡的喜悅。


## CHAPTER 25｜The Courage to Be Wrong｜敢於犯錯的勇氣

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：874**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Sera refused to begin with the official conclusion. When a new warning arrives from the frontier, Sera posts its assumptions openly and invites a dozen rival explanations. In the imperial observatory, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。瑟拉拒絕從官方結論開始。邊境傳來新警報時，瑟拉公開預測前提，邀請十幾種不同解釋。在帝國觀星臺，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Sera asked Tarin to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。瑟拉請塔林說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Amid the waiting crowd, Maelin saw families from all three territories reading the corrected treaty together. The anticipation of a final judgment did not erase the damage already done to the villages. A former apprentice testified about the replacement plate without being blamed for every decision made by his employer. The charge did not allege an attempt to assassinate anyone, and the judges refused to invent one. Keir said the community's backbone was its willingness to preserve records even when doing so embarrassed officials. Sera asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 在等候人群之中，瑟拉看見三地家庭一起閱讀更正後的條約。 等待最終判決的期待，沒有抹去村莊已承受的損害。 一名前學徒就替換印版作證，沒有被迫承擔雇主的全部決策。 指控沒有涉及企圖暗殺任何人，法官拒絕憑空增添罪名。 凱爾說社區的支柱是願意保存紀錄，即使這讓官員難堪。 瑟拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. She writes beneath the map: a future we can change is not a prophecy of defeat. Sera felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。她在地圖下寫下：能被改變的未來，不等於失敗的預言。瑟拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Nessa used the archived accounts to compute the money owed to families whose grain had been seized. The court found no corpse connected with the alleged language fraud, despite rumors spread to inflame the public. A witness using a crutch was given a chair near the entrance rather than being forced to stand. The unlawful detention of several travelers was reviewed separately from the treaty wording offense. Maelin hoped to dwell near the river after losing the royal office she had once considered her identity. Tarin marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 妮莎利用封存帳目計算被徵糧家庭應獲補償的金額。 法庭確認語言造假指控與任何屍體無關，儘管有人散布流言煽動大眾。 一位拄拐杖的證人獲安排在入口附近坐下，沒有被迫站立。 幾名旅客遭非法拘留的案件，與條約文字犯罪分案審理。 失去曾視為身分象徵的王室職位後，瑟拉希望住在河邊。 塔林標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Marshal Dorian and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Sera rejected both. The issue was whether fear of disaster justifies the empire’s wars, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住多里安元帥的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑟拉兩者都拒絕。核心是對災難的恐懼是否足以合理化帝國發動戰爭，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The crowd became frantic for a single villain, but the court continued to examine each person's conduct. A hostel offered rooms to the displaced families who had come to collect their compensation decisions. A knowledgeable village teacher helped children compare the three corrected language columns. The judge noted that the altered clause had been dangerous but was not itself a lethal weapon. A lizard warmed itself on a stone wall, and for a moment the street seemed wonderfully ordinary. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 群眾急著找出唯一惡人，法庭仍逐一審視每人的行為。 一間青年旅舍提供房間給前來領取補償裁定的流離家庭。 一名博學的村莊老師協助孩子比對三欄更正後的語文。 法官指出遭改條款非常危險，卻不因此把它當作致命武器。 一隻蜥蜴在石牆上曬太陽，整條街忽然顯得平凡得美好。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The remainder of the restitution fund would be held for families whose losses required additional proof. The verdict seemed to stun the crowd when Maelin was suspended but not convicted of treason. Three successive public readings confirmed that none of the corrected clauses concealed another transfer of ownership. The treasury was ordered to return the diverted printing funds and publish a separate account of compensation. Maelin felt a slight vibration through the wooden desk as the final register was closed. The village teacher explained how one missing vowel had once transformed a promise into a threat. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 賠償基金餘款將保留給損失仍須補證的家庭。 瑟拉遭停職卻未被判叛國，判決似乎讓群眾震驚。 連續三次公開宣讀確認，更正條款沒有再暗藏所有權轉移。 國庫被命令返還挪用的印製款，並另行公布補償帳目。 最後登記冊闔上時，瑟拉感到木桌傳來輕微震動。 村莊老師解釋，一個遺失的母音曾如何讓承諾變成威脅。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Sera had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Tarin wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Marshal Dorian. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑟拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。塔林把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起多里安元帥注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

**EN** Sera did not call the day a perfect victory. She writes beneath the map: a future we can change is not a prophecy of defeat. What endured was a decision that the people of the imperial observatory could challenge, revise, and make their own. Tarin placed a cracked prophecy lens beside the public record. They left the last page open for names not yet written.

**繁中** 瑟拉不把這一天稱為完美勝利。她在地圖下寫下：能被改變的未來，不等於失敗的預言。真正留下的是帝國觀星臺人民有權質疑、修改並共同擁有的決定。塔林把一枚裂開的預言透鏡放在公共紀錄旁。他們把最後一頁留白，等待未來尚未寫上的姓名。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. When a new warning arrives from the frontier, Sera posts its assumptions openly and invites a dozen rival explanations.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 邊境傳來新警報時，瑟拉公開預測前提，邀請十幾種不同解釋。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. She writes beneath the map: a future we can change is not a prophecy of defeat.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 她在地圖下寫下：能被改變的未來，不等於失敗的預言。
